- **Open.** (T) A handler that creates another error, or reenters ACE, while the retained pError of an outer completion event is alive (the sink cannot make calls); ERR-NEST's native callback cases need R-UDF-1.

### R-ERR-1 ERR-CLOSE: duplicate Close and held-child calls

- **Claim.** `Close` on an already closed Database or Recordset, a member call on a closed object, and calls on a held child after the parent closed give 3420 ("Object invalid or no longer set", source the object's class) and one entry in `DBEngine.Errors`; a second Database on the same file keeps working after the first closes; a held Error keeps its payload after `Database.Close` (ERR-SNAPSHOT); an enumerator over a collection of the closed Database returns 3420 from `Next`.
- **Conditions.** DAO raw lane, ACCDB.
- **Records.** e1b/closed-db-openrs, closed-rs-close-twice, closed-rs-movenext; d1e/life-database-workspace-close, err-snapshot-held; def/recordset-closed-member-inventory; q/errsnap-held-variants; q2/enum-close-during-walk.
- **Open.** (T) Close from a callback, reentry into an ancestor, cleanup failure after a primary error.

### R-ERR-1 ERR-PARTIAL: constraint refusals under dbFailOnError change nothing; lock refusals are page-granular and keep earlier updates; without the flag violating rows are skipped silently

- **Claim.** Constraint refusals: `Database.Execute` of an INSERT with `dbFailOnError` (128) that violates a primary key or unique index (3022), a required field (3314), a zero-length required Text (3315), a relationship (3201), a field or table rule (3316) or a type conversion (3464) leaves the table unchanged, `RecordsAffected` 0, one Errors entry; an `INSERT ... SELECT` with a duplicate in the first, middle or last row gives 3022 and adds no rows; without the flag the same statement inserts the other rows and raises no error. `DELETE FROM PA` (some parent has a child) with 128 gives 3200, RecordsAffected 0 and PA unchanged, whichever parent (first, third, fifth of five) carries the child; without 128 it raises no error, RecordsAffected is 4 and the row with the child is the only one left. `UPDATE T SET ID = ID + 1` against a relationship gives 3200 with no row changed. A byte value of 300 inserted into a Byte column is accepted and stored as 44. Lock refusals (another Workspace holds a pessimistic edit): the lock covers the page of the edited row (about 18 rows of the 2048-row test table). On the 8-row, one-page table an `UPDATE` gives 3188 with 128 and changes no row, and changes no row, raises no error and reports 0 without the flag. On the 2048-row table, `UPDATE Big SET V = 'u'` with a lock on ID 1, 1000 or 2048: without the flag rows of the other pages are updated and the locked page's rows are skipped, no error, RecordsAffected 2030, 2030 and 2034 (18, 18 and 14 rows skipped; MIN updated ID 19 for the lock on ID 1, MAX 2034 for the lock on ID 2048); WITH 128 the statement fails 3188 with RecordsAffected 0, stops at the first row on the locked page and KEEPS the earlier updates in key order: 0 rows for the lock on ID 1, IDs 1-990 for the lock on ID 1000, IDs 1-2034 for the lock on ID 2048. Recordset `Update` violations keep EditMode and the buffer (CUR-EDIT).
- **Conditions.** ACCDB, one Database (second Workspace for the lock cases); table read back through a snapshot; the lock statements update in key order.
- **Records.** q2/errpart-lock-multipage-failonerror, errpart-lock-multipage-plain (discriminating: lock on the first, a middle and the last page, flag on and off; settles page granularity and the missing rollback); q/errpart-delete-ri-position-failonerror, errpart-delete-ri-position-plain, errpart-lock-position; fix/con-pk-duplicate-insert-failonerror, con-unique-index-insert-failonerror, con-required-omitted-failonerror, con-zero-length-required-failonerror, con-fk-orphan-insert-failonerror, con-field-validation-insert-failonerror, con-table-validation-insert-failonerror, sql-insert-text-into-number-failonerror, ovf-insert-byte-failonerror, err-multi-violation-sql-failonerror, proto-txn-fail-rollback-failonerror; d1e/err-partial-constraint (first/middle/last, with and without 128); e1b/con-fk-parent-delete.
- **Open.** (T) I/O, flush and delivery faults, killed process (R-CRASH-1 and fault injection; r-sample-rec shows that an explicit transaction writes new data pages first and pages 0, 1 and 45-48 later, and that an implicit INSERT is written after it returned, but records no failure). The file hash changed after a last-row duplicate rollback with no row change (d1e) is unexplained.

## R-EXPR-1, R-SQL-1 to R-SQL-3 and R-PAR-1

### R-EXPR-1 Branch A: Null propagation and parameter input adaptation
**Claim.** In a SELECT expression `Null + x`, `-Null`, `Not Null`, `Null = x` and `Null <> x` are Null, while `&` treats Null as an empty string (`Null & 'x'` = "x", `Null & Null` is Null). `IsNull('')` is 0 and `Len(Null)` is Null. A declared Long parameter takes Null as Null, Empty as 0 and the VT_ERROR "missing" marker as Null; a declared or undeclared Text parameter reads Null for Null, Empty, the marker and an empty BSTR. An undeclared parameter reads back as Text whatever its input tag (I4 7 gives "7", R8 2.5 gives "2.5", DATE 0.25 gives "06:00:00"). A BSTR "q" or "" put into a declared Long parameter fails at the put (3421).
**Conditions.** DAO QueryDef.Parameters, snapshot, ACCDB; declared types Long and Text and undeclared names.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/a-null-empty-error-literals/r-expr-1.a-null-empty-error-literals.f2882098b8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/a-parameter-inputs/r-expr-1.a-parameter-inputs.d375f6c653.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/b-plus-versus-concat/r-expr-1.b-plus-versus-concat.963f485c1c.a1.json`; independent repeat: `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/nz-free-forms/r-fix-expr-sql.nz-free-forms.4105e37dd6.a1.json` (Long p Null gives Null, Empty gives 0), `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/parameters-values-set/r-fix-expr-sql.parameters-values-set.55c1458fde.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-null-and-unset/r-sql-13b.params-null-and-unset.71e0e47568.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, optional arguments).** Fed through a declared Long parameter p, Null into an optional argument is refused and Empty behaves as an explicit 0: `Round(2.5, p)` and `Left('abcd', p)` with p Null fail 3464 at OpenRecordset, `Mid('abcd', 2, p)` and `InStr(p, 'abcd', 'c')` with p Null fail 94 "Invalid use of Null"; with p Empty (read back as Long 0) `Round(2.5, p)` is 2, `Mid('abcd', 2, p)` and `Left('abcd', p)` are "" and `InStr(p, 'abcd', 'c')` fails 5 "Invalid procedure call", the same as p = 0. Omission differs: `Round(2.5)` is 2, `Mid('abcd', 2)` is "bcd", `InStr('abcd', 'c')` is 3.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/a-optarg-null-empty-params/r-q-expr-sql.a-optarg-null-empty-params.822ae8f57e.a1.json`.
**Open.** No Error-valued input exists (CVErr is undefined, see the availability note); Error values, arrays and objects need an Access/VBA host (status T). An explicit Error marker as optional argument was not asked.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch A, EXPR-INVENTORY, EXPR-DOMAIN, EXPR-EVAL: function availability in the standalone expression service
**Claim.** In standalone DAO/ACE, `Nz`, `CVErr`, `IsError`, `IsArray`, `Eval`, `Environ`, `CurDir`, `Dir`, `FileLen`, `Command`, `Join`, `Split`, `Filter`, `UBound`, `Randomize`, `Log10`, `CurrentUser()`, `CurrentDb()`, `CLngLng`, `CLngPtr`, the file-handle functions and one-argument `CDec` are not available: calls fail with 0x800A0C0D (3085 "Undefined function") or 0x800A0C03 for CDec. `DLookup`, `DSum`, `DCount`, `DMax`, `DMin`, `DFirst`, `DLast`, `DAvg` work and return their result to DAO as Text for DMax/DMin/DFirst/DLast/DLookup. A context capability result, not a defect: Access-hosted values cannot be created here.
**Conditions.** DAO SELECT/Filter/Sort expressions on ACCDB and MDB (`expr-clnglng-mdb`).
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1b/sandbox-vba-library/r-expr-1b.sandbox-vba-library.55bf0b9648.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/sandbox-system-and-file-functions/r-expr-1b.sandbox-system-and-file-functions.ae73124ef1.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/sandbox-access-hosted-names/r-expr-1b.sandbox-access-hosted-names.8ac7ffb162.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-filehandle-functions-unopened/r-def-expr-sql.expr-filehandle-functions-unopened.de799c3d70.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-clnglng-clngptr/r-def-expr-sql.expr-clnglng-clngptr.07c24b48b6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-clnglng-mdb/r-def-expr-sql.expr-clnglng-mdb.8f2eb5233a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/cdec-argument-forms/r-fix-expr-sql.cdec-argument-forms.952cd70a13.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/array-functions-split-only/r-fix-expr-sql.array-functions-split-only.0b22e64004.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/host-routes-domain-eval/r-expr-1.host-routes-domain-eval.2274c94294.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-domain-aggregate-values/r-def-expr-sql.expr-domain-aggregate-values.1f10ca6730.a1.json`.
**Open.** `Array(...)` crashes ACE and is never asked; `CDec(1,2)` is admitted and returns Long 0 (unexplained). Access-hosted routes are out of scope here.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: text against number comparison
**Claim.** A comparison of a number with text that is not a number (`7 < 'q'`, `7 = '7'`, `'10' < 7`, a stored Long against a stored Text) opens, and fails with 0x800A0D88 on the Value read; comparing two declared parameters of Long and Text fails at OpenRecordset. Two Text values compare as text (`'7' < '10'` is 0). Undeclared parameters carry text, so `[ua] = [ub]` compares as text ((7,"7") equal, (10,"9") less). `#date# = 'text'` returns 0 with no error. `WHERE T = N` over stored columns fails at open, per row for the projection form.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-numeric-text-compare/r-expr-1.b-numeric-text-compare.4560e822e0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-cross-types/r-expr-1b.cmp-cross-types.e898adce72.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-stored-lanes/r-expr-1b.cmp-stored-lanes.269e15de0b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/stored-where-per-row/r-fix-expr-sql.stored-where-per-row.cf6792983b.a1.json`.
**Open.** Date/Time Extended; the direct VBA lane.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: addition against concatenation
**Claim.** `+` with a number and numeric text adds (`7 + '7'` is Double 14); `+` of two texts concatenates (`'7' + '7'` = "77"); `7 + 'q'` fails with 0x800A0D88 on Value. `+` with Null or Empty is Null; `&` always concatenates, treats Null as empty and formats a Boolean as "-1" and a date as "2024/01/01". `N + T` over stored 7 and '7' is Double 14 (but `N = T` fails).
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB, literals, stored columns and undeclared parameters.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-plus-versus-concat/r-expr-1.b-plus-versus-concat.963f485c1c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/op-plus-amp-precedence-and-types/r-expr-1b.op-plus-amp-precedence-and-types.674d84115d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/op-concat-number-forms/r-expr-1b.op-concat-number-forms.94303d4918.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-stored-lanes/r-expr-1b.cmp-stored-lanes.269e15de0b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/stored-null-iif/r-fix-expr-sql.stored-null-iif.2f97406e25.a1.json` (`T & 'x'` "x", `T + 'x'` Null for a stored Null).

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: integer widening and overflow
**Claim.** Byte and Integer arithmetic widens: `B + CByte(1)` is VT_I4 256, `CInt(100) * CInt(400)` is 40000, `CByte(200) + CByte(100)` is 300. Long arithmetic does not widen: `L + CLng(1)` at the maximum, `L * CLng(2)` and `-CLng(-2147483648)` fail with 0x800A0006 on the Value read; integer literals widen to Double (`2147483647 + 1` is 2147483648). BIGINT (ACCDB only) adds like Long: `B + 1`, `B * 2` at the extremes overflow on Value, `B \ 2` stays BIGINT, `B / 2` is a Double, `B + 1.5` fails 3464 on rows at the maximum.
**Conditions.** ACCDB stored columns and literals; BIGINT is refused in MDB (3001).
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-integer-widening/r-expr-1.b-integer-widening.52ba244d05.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/arith-overflow-promotion/r-expr-1b.arith-overflow-promotion.8e33d497ff.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/bigint-stored-arith/r-fix-expr-sql.bigint-stored-arith.39720af343.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/bigint-stored-compare-convert/r-fix-expr-sql.bigint-stored-compare-convert.30231878df.a1.json`.
**Open.** `B+1.5` on ID 3 returning I8 6 is unexplained.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: rounding in integer conversion, `\` and `Mod`
**Claim.** CInt, CLng and CByte round half to even: 2.5 gives 2, 3.5 gives 4, -2.5 gives -2, 0.5 gives 0, 100.5 gives 100, 101.5 gives 102; 2.5000000000000004 gives 3. They fail with 0x800A0006 outside the range (`CInt(32767.5)`, `CByte(255.5)`, `CByte(-0.6)`). `\` and `Mod` round their operands first (`7.5 \ 2` is 4, `7 \ 2.5` is 3, `7 \ 0.4` fails 0x800A000B) and `/` always gives Double; `1 / 0` fails 0x800A000B on Value, `0 / 0` fails 0x800A0006. CCur rounds half to even at four decimals (0.00015 and 0.00025 give 0.0002, 0.00005 gives 0); `Round(0.125,2)` is 0.12.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB, literals and stored Double.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-integer-conversion/r-expr-1.b-integer-conversion.4a417fa48a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/integer-conversion-alias/r-fix-expr-sql.integer-conversion-alias.60068f498b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/b-intdiv-mod/r-expr-1.b-intdiv-mod.60456b370a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-half-rounding-large/r-expr-1b.conv-half-rounding-large.11d003a414.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-range-edges/r-expr-1b.conv-range-edges.01b85c3b66.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/arith-intdiv-mod-types/r-expr-1b.arith-intdiv-mod-types.dee0e58c4e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-round-function-digits/r-expr-1b.conv-round-function-digits.8f79dca0d3.a1.json`.
**Open.** Text boundary forms are in the text-to-number note.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: mixed-type result types and Currency/Decimal fidelity
**Claim.** Currency + Double and Double + Currency are Currency; Currency * Double is Double; Single with Long or Double is Double; Currency + Long is Currency; Date + number is Date; Date - Date is Double. A decimal-point literal (`1.5`) is Decimal (VarType 14) although the DAO Field.Type of an expression can differ from the inner VarType (`Abs(-2.5)` is Field type 7 with VarType 14; `Abs(CInt(-32768))` is R8 with VarType 3). A stored Decimal(28,4) with magnitude 2^64+5 survives identity, unary minus, `+0`, `*1`, `/1`: -1844674407370955.1621; Decimal + Currency, + Long, * Long stay Decimal; Decimal + Double is Field type 7 with VarType 14; CCur and `\ 1` of it overflow (6) on Value; CDbl gives R8. Currency maximum and minimum round-trip exactly through a parameter and overflow on one-unit neighbours at OpenRecordset.
**Conditions.** ACCDB; the Decimal column is made by ADO DDL with explicit precision and scale.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-mixed-precedence/r-expr-1.b-mixed-precedence.b8da1929d2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/dec-mixed-precedence/r-fix-expr-sql.dec-mixed-precedence.ec3865446f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/dec-fixed-point-fidelity/r-fix-expr-sql.dec-fixed-point-fidelity.71d4fc7f5f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/meta-conversion-field-types/r-expr-1b.meta-conversion-field-types.bef199cacc.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/info-result-vartype-numeric/r-expr-1b.info-result-vartype-numeric.2ca10df6b5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/b-fixed-point-fidelity/r-expr-1.b-fixed-point-fidelity.43044a79c9.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, Decimal scale).** A stored Decimal keeps its own scale on read: Decimal(28,4) 1.5000, Decimal(28,20) 1.50000000000000000000, Decimal(28,28) 0.0000000000000000000000000001 and 0.5; VarType 14 for all. Equal values of different scale compare equal (`a = b` is -1 for 1.5000 and 1.5 at scale 20; `WHERE a = b` selects all three rows) and `WHERE c = 0.5` selects the right row. `c + c` stays Decimal at scale 28 (1E-28 + 1E-28 gives 2E-28, 0.5 + 0.5 gives 1.0000000000000000000000000000); CStr(c) is exact and CDbl(c) is 1.0000000000000001E-28; Sum(c) is Decimal 0.5000000000000000000000000003 and Sum(a) Decimal 3.5001. Arithmetic between Decimal columns of different scale (`a + b`, `a * b`, `a - b`) returns Double (3, 2.25, 0), and `c * 1` returns Double, whereas `M * 1` on the Decimal(28,4) column of the first fixture stayed Decimal: whether a Decimal result survives depends on the scales involved, and the rule is not determined.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/dec-scale-compare/r-q-expr-sql.dec-scale-compare.3496819056.a1.json`.
**Open.** Why mixed-scale arithmetic and `c * 1` go Double (same-scale `a + a`, `a * a` were not asked); Decimal typed parameters (the builder has no VT_DECIMAL argument).

### R-EXPR-1 Branch B: floating-point comparison fidelity
**Claim.** Doubles compare exactly: 0.1 and 0.10000000000000002 as parameters are unequal and their difference is -1.3877787807814457E-17; Single 1.1 widened equals Double 1.1 only in the Single value (difference 2.3841857821338408E-08); `0.1 + 0.2 = 0.3` is -1. Non-finite and signed-zero parameter values arrive as text ("nan", "inf", "-0") and the non-finite ones fail 0x800A0D88 at OpenRecordset.
**Conditions.** DAO parameters (declared Double and undeclared), literals, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/b-floating-fidelity/r-expr-1.b-floating-fidelity.c580cdca94.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/eq-cross-type/r-sql-1.eq-cross-type.6be1a0daf9.a1.json`.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch C: DATE payloads
**Claim.** A DATE parameter and a Double parameter with the same payload behave identically in comparison and CDbl (raw payloads -1.25, -1.5, -0.25 are preserved: `CDbl(DateSerial(1899,12,29))` is -1); comparison, BETWEEN and ORDER BY follow the numeric payload (BETWEEN #12/29/1899# AND #12/31/1899# selects the same rows as the numeric range). Component functions read negative payloads as time-of-day plus day offset (`Hour(-0.25)` 6, `Day(-0.25)` 30). `CDate('1/2/2003')` is 37623 in en-ZA, impossible dates (`'2/30/2003'`) and out-of-range numbers fail 0x800A0D88; `#d# + 0.5` is Decimal.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, en-ZA locale (parse results are locale-bound).
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/c-date-payloads/r-expr-1.c-date-payloads.28a1b72090.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/c-date-stored-predicates/r-expr-1.c-date-stored-predicates.b2dd8fd9cd.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/c-date-text-and-literals/r-expr-1.c-date-text-and-literals.0cd9f9bf25.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-cdate-forms/r-expr-1b.conv-cdate-forms.d5a3fec393.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/date-serial-edges/r-expr-1b.date-serial-edges.98fc78ab88.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/expr-literals-date-forms/r-def-expr-sql.expr-literals-date-forms.0073d8d417.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, index).** An index does not change the result. Over the payloads -1.5, -1.25, -0.25, 0, 0.25, 1.25, 45000.5, `D = CDate(-0.25)` selects the -0.25 row; `D = #12/30/1899 06:00#` selects the +0.25 row (that literal is +0.25); `D BETWEEN #12/29/1899# AND #12/31/1899#` selects -0.25, 0 and 0.25; `D < 0`, `D > -1.3`, `CDbl(D) = -0.25` (residual only) and `ORDER BY D` give the same ids and the numeric order on a table without an index and on one with an index on D built before the insert.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/c-date-index-vs-residual/r-q-expr-sql.c-date-index-vs-residual.c8a5048ad5.a1.json`.
**Open.** A second locale; Date/Time Extended; undeclared DATE parameters read back as text, so DATE-parameter arithmetic needs a declared DateTime parameter.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch D: logical and bitwise operators and their consumers
**Claim.** And/Or/Xor/Eqv/Imp/Not over True/False/Null follow Kleene-style rules (And: T,F,N,F,F,F,N,F,N in the grid order TT,TF,TN,FT,FF,FN,NT,NF,NN; `Null And 0` is 0, `Null Or -1` is -1); on non-Boolean integers they are logical, not bitwise: `6 And 3`, `6 Or 3`, `6 Eqv 3`, `6 Imp 3` and `5 And 3` are VT_I2 -1 (a bitwise And would be 2), `6 Xor 3`, `5 Xor 3` and `Not 6` are 0. A WHERE predicate selects the row for any non-Null non-zero numeric (1, -1, 2, 2.5), for '' and for the texts '1', 'q', 'True'; it rejects Null, 0 and '0'. DAO FindFirst accepts only a comparison criterion: on a fresh dynaset `Null` gives NoMatch True without error, and the bare values 0, 1, -1, 2, 2.5, '', '1', '0', 'q', 'True' each fail with 3001 "Invalid argument"; `CVErr(5)` fails 3072.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot/dynaset, ACCDB; FindFirst asked on a fresh recordset per criterion.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/d-logical-bitwise/r-expr-1.d-logical-bitwise.952f06510f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/d-predicate-consumers/r-expr-1.d-predicate-consumers.cac6cb9e52.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/findfirst-fresh-recordset/r-fix-expr-sql.findfirst-fresh-recordset.eae4a4c21a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cmp-between-in/r-expr-1b.cmp-between-in.059527a48b.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, validation).** And and Or as a field validation rule follow the three-valued tables, and the row is admitted when the rule is True or Null: `[V] > 0 And [V] < 10` admits 5 and Null and rejects -1, 15 and -7; `[V] > 0 Or [V] < -5` admits 5, 15, -7 and Null and rejects -1; `([V] > 0) And Null` admits 5, 15 and Null (True And Null is Null) and rejects -1 and -7 (False And Null is False). A rejection is 3317 (no ValidationText set) with EditMode 2.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/d-validation-andor/r-q-expr-sql.d-validation-andor.14034a8da7.a1.json`.
**Open.** And/Or in default expressions; Error-valued predicates need a host route.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-MUTATION, Branch D: validation rules and Required
**Claim.** A field validation rule that evaluates False rejects the Update with 3316 (EditMode stays 2); a rule that evaluates Null admits the row, so an omitted or Null value passes `[V] > 0`; Required rejects Null independently (3314); an evaluation error rejects with 3802 "Error evaluating CHECK constraint" at Update. A table rule `[V] < [R]` rejects (2,1) with 3316 and admits (1,NULL); a table rule `Null` admits every row. A bare-literal field rule reads as an equality against the field: rule `0` admits only V = 0, `2` only V = 2, `True` only V = -1, `False` only V = 0; `''`, `'1'`, `'q'` admit no non-Null value; the rule text reads back with a trailing space.
**Conditions.** DAO table-type AddNew/Update, ACCDB; fresh recordset and fresh table per case in the repaired probe.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/mut-validation-rules/r-expr-1.mut-validation-rules.9d6f3c59df.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/validation-rule-literals/r-fix-expr-sql.validation-rule-literals.e7c3545cec.a1.json`.

**Correction.** The number of a rule rejection depends on ValidationText: 3316 when the field's ValidationText is set (the message is that text), 3317 ("One or more values are prohibited by the validation rule '...'") when it is not (`evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-declaration-order/r-q-expr-sql.mut-declaration-order.9f1e2cbf5f.a1.json` against `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/d-validation-andor/r-q-expr-sql.d-validation-andor.14034a8da7.a1.json` and `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/validation-rule-literals/r-fix-expr-sql.validation-rule-literals.e7c3545cec.a1.json`). The "3316" in the Claim above is therefore the with-text number; the records of r-expr-1 were not re-checked for which form they used. Both numbers are the same rejection.
**Claim (follow-up).** With two invalid fields the message reported is that of the field declared first, whichever field is assigned first: MA declares A, B and reports "A must be positive" for assignment orders A,B and B,A; MB declares B, A and reports "B must be positive" in both orders. A rule that names another field is refused at field level: setting `[V] < [R]` as the V field rule fails at the property put with 3783 "cannot use multiple columns in a column-level CHECK constraint" (so (2,1), (1,2) and (1,Null) are all accepted); the same text as TableDef.ValidationRule is accepted, rejects (2,1) with 3317 and admits (1,2) and (1,Null). A rejected insert still consumes an AutoNumber (ID 4 is missing).
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-declaration-order/r-q-expr-sql.mut-declaration-order.9f1e2cbf5f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-field-rule-other-field/r-q-expr-sql.mut-field-rule-other-field.47e42cc479.a1.json`.
**Open.** AllowZeroLength as a separate variable; restricted-UDF rules need a host route.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-MUTATION, EXPR-STAGE: ValidateOnSet timing and defaults
**Claim.** With ValidateOnSet True an invalid assignment fails at the Value put (3316, source DAO.Field), the field keeps its value and EditMode is 1; with False the put succeeds (the invalid value reads back) and Update fails (3316, source DAO.Recordset); CancelUpdate restores the old value. With two invalid fields the V message is returned in both assignment orders. A DefaultValue `2+3` is visible after AddNew as VT_I4 5; `1/0` fails at the property set (0x800A000B) and `'q'` on a Long field fails 0x800A000D; an explicit Null assignment persists as Null and SQL INSERT omitting the column stores the default.
**Conditions.** DAO table recordsets, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/stage-validate-on-set/r-expr-1.stage-validate-on-set.6a9e23dbb6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/mut-default-values/r-expr-1.mut-default-values.bf7d388dd2.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, clock and random defaults).** A DefaultValue of `Now()` or `Rnd()` is sampled when AddNew is called and held until Update: T and R read the same 2.1 s after AddNew, the stored row equals the AddNew values, a second AddNew samples again (new T, Rnd 0.5795 against 0.5334), and a further AddNew after CancelUpdate samples again (Rnd 0.2896; T equals the cancelled AddNew's because the clock second had not advanced). Reading T and R after Update fails (no current record).
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-default-now-rnd/r-q-expr-sql.mut-default-now-rnd.39f98e5c75.a1.json`.
**Open.** Transaction and multi-process interplay belongs to R-CURSOR-1/R-TXN-1; restricted-UDF defaults need a host route.

### R-EXPR-1 Branch E: conditional functions and selection rules
**Claim.** IIf evaluates only the selected arm for division by zero and text conversion failures: `IIf(True,1,1/0)` is R8 1, `IIf(False,1/0,2)` is 2, `IIf(True,1,CInt('q'))` is 1, also with row-dependent `1/[Denominator]` at Denominator 0. Switch and Choose fail at OpenRecordset when any constant arm fails (`Switch(True,1,True,1/0)`, `Choose(1,'a',1/0)` 0x800A000B). In `1/0 + CInt('q')` the first operand's error is reported and reversing reports the other. IIf: Null condition selects the false arm, `IIf('',1,2)` is 1, `IIf(True,1,'a')` is Text "1"; `IIf(True)` fails 0x800A0C03. Switch with no true pair is Null; `Switch(True)` and `Switch(True,1,False)` fail 0x800A0C03; Choose with index 0, 3, 0.5, -0.5 is Null, 1.5 gives arm 1, 2.5 arm 2 (round half to even), Null index fails 0x800A0D88. Nesting 10 IIf deep works; 20 fails "too complex".
**Conditions.** DAO, constants and one row-dependent IIf; ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/e-eager-failures/r-expr-1.e-eager-failures.c9c723c692.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/e-conditional-selection/r-expr-1.e-conditional-selection.a85f932a4b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/e-failure-phase/r-expr-1.e-failure-phase.6279e4757f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cond-iif-forms/r-expr-1b.cond-iif-forms.697f48b067.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/cond-switch-choose-forms/r-expr-1b.cond-switch-choose-forms.393479eddd.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, rows).** With Ev (Id, Denominator, TextValue) = (1,5,'1'), (2,0,'x'), (3,2,'7'): `Switch(True,1,True,1/[Denominator])` and `Choose(1,'a',1/[Denominator])` fail on the Value read of row 2 although the first arm is selected, and succeed on rows 1 and 3, so Switch and Choose evaluate every value argument per row and a division by zero in an unselected arm surfaces. The error reported is the leftmost failing argument's, in Switch, Choose and `+`: `Switch(Id = 2, CInt([TextValue]), Id = 2, 1/[Denominator])` reports 3464 and the reversed order reports 11; the same for `Choose(1, ...)` and `CInt(...) + 1/...`. `IIf(Id = 2, X, Y)` reports the selected arm's failure (1/0 gives 11, CInt('x') gives 3464). With `WHERE Id = 999` Switch and Choose open with no rows and no error.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/e-row-arms/r-q-expr-sql.e-row-arms.583c2c52e5.a1.json`.
**Open.** IIf with an unselected failing arm in a row rests on the earlier record only (`IIf(True,1,1/[Denominator])` on Denominator 0 gives 1); marker UDF and object default-member order need a host route; the nesting limit between 10 and 20 is not bracketed.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-STAGE, R-SQL-1 SQL-DEMAND, R-PAR-1 PAR-ERROR: where a row error surfaces
**Claim.** Argument-count and parse errors, an undefined function, a failing expression that every aggregate, GROUP BY, DISTINCT, UNION or ORDER BY key must read, and a constant failing expression (`1/0`, `CByte(300)`) fail at OpenRecordset. A failing projected scalar on one row fails only the Value read of that field (1/[Denominator], `CInt([TextValue])`, `1/zm`): OpenRecordset, MoveNext, MoveLast, EOF and RecordCount succeed and neighbouring rows read normally; re-reading the row or moving back onto it raises again. A WHERE that matches no row opens with no rows. A failing WHERE term makes MoveNext fail (3071) at the bad row and the row is absent. A failing projection beyond a TOP cutoff raises nothing. GetRows stops silently before a bad projected row (a short batch, no error). The behaviour is identical for snapshot, dynaset and forward-only recordsets and on MDB.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, dynaset and forward-only; ACCDB and MDB (`err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step-mdb`); ER fixture of 240 rows, bad rows at ids 3, 60, 80, 100, 150, 240.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/e-failure-phase/r-expr-1.e-failure-phase.6279e4757f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step/r-par-1.err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step.75174593c2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step-mdb/r-par-1.err-snapshot-mid-bad-row-step-mdb.a8915d290f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-dynaset-mid-bad-row-step/r-par-1.err-dynaset-mid-bad-row-step.f8bad47fee.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-forwardonly-mid-bad-row-step/r-par-1.err-forwardonly-mid-bad-row-step.ba94fa4faa.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-revisit-bad-row/r-par-1.err-revisit-bad-row.3ddc406403.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-getrows-chunks/r-par-1.err-getrows-chunks.c24e55f2b8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-two-errors-two-rows/r-par-1.err-two-errors-two-rows.8d5129947d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-expression-kinds-phase/r-par-1.err-expression-kinds-phase.0bfd412e3f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/err-last-bad-row-bounded/r-fix-expr-sql.err-last-bad-row-bounded.0ef74b606a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/err-aggregate-attributed/r-fix-expr-sql.err-aggregate-attributed.7a37b263e1.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/err-where-order-attributed/r-fix-expr-sql.err-where-order-attributed.c876ba4187.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-top-stops-before-bad-row/r-par-1.err-top-stops-before-bad-row.db1df3b225.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/demand-conversion/r-sql-1.demand-conversion.a4bbf6a923.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/top-demand/r-sql-2-3.top-demand.a543dfd216.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, saved QueryDefs).** The phases hold through a saved QueryDef opened with QueryDef.OpenRecordset (Bad: z = 0 at id 3 of 5). A projected `1/z` opens, fails only on the Value read of row 3, MoveLast and RecordCount (5) succeed, rows 4 and 5 read; with ORDER BY id DESC the same. `WHERE 1/z > 0` opens, the MoveNext onto the bad row fails and the row is absent (RecordCount 4). `Sum(1/z)` and a crosstab with `PIVOT 1/z` fail at open. `TOP 2 ... ORDER BY id` (cutoff before the bad row) raises nothing; `TOP 4` fails on the Value read of row 3.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/demand-saved-querydefs/r-q-expr-sql.demand-saved-querydefs.03c2aee28d.a1.json`.
**Open.** Requery after an error (crashes ACE), an unread speculative page error and aggregate-before-callback order stay T. The Errors reads after MoveLast in that record show stale entries: a successful call neither clears nor adds Errors.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-INVENTORY: text to number conversion grammar
**Claim.** CInt/CLng/CByte accept `' 12 '`, `'1e2'`(100), `'&H10'`(16), `'&O17'`(15), `'+5'`, `'1.5E+2'`(150) and `'1 2'`(12); `'(12)'` converts to -12; `'&HFFFF'` is -1 for CInt and 65535 for CLng; `'1,234'`, `'12abc'`, `''`, `'$12'` and full-width digits fail 0x800A0D88; CDbl of `'1E+400'` overflows (6), `'1,5'`, `'NaN'`, `'Infinity'` fail 0x800A0D88. `Val('1d3')` is 1000, `Val('&HFFFF')` is -1, `Val(Null)` fails 0x800A0D88. Null arguments: CInt(Null) and `Mid('a',1,Null)` fail 0x800A005E; `Abs/Int/Fix/Round/Trim/UCase/Hex/Oct(Null)` and `InStr(Null,'a')` return Null.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, en-ZA, one expression per snapshot.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-text-to-integer/r-expr-1b.conv-text-to-integer.b0637e2413.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-text-to-floating/r-expr-1b.conv-text-to-floating.3213e9b55c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/conv-val-forms/r-expr-1b.conv-val-forms.614ad20e3c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/null-propagation-functions/r-expr-1b.null-propagation-functions.b2d04aed9f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/a-optional-arguments/r-expr-1.a-optional-arguments.7b1e2e3cf6.a1.json`.
**Open.** Locale-bound results (en-ZA only). This is a sample of the function manifest, not the manifest.

### R-EXPR-1 EXPR-RUNTIME: Rnd and Now sampling in queries
**Claim.** In one query execution `Rnd()` returns the same value in every row; different executions give different values; `Rnd(Id)` differs per row; `Rnd(0)` repeats the preceding `Rnd()` in the same row; `Now()` is identical across rows and columns. `Rnd(Null)` fails 0x800A005E.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB, no `Randomize`.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/runtime-rnd-now/r-expr-1.runtime-rnd-now.de5f5b536a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/rnd-seeded-sequences/r-expr-1b.rnd-seeded-sequences.4cc9f2ea4b.a1.json`.

**Records (follow-up).** Field defaults of Now() and Rnd() are in the ValidateOnSet and defaults note (`evidence/r-q-expr-sql/mut-default-now-rnd/r-q-expr-sql.mut-default-now-rnd.39f98e5c75.a1.json`).
**Open.** VBA interleaving and a forced physical replan need a host route.

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-01: LIKE wildcard grammar by route
**Claim.** DAO SQL `LIKE` uses the Access grammar: `*` and `?` are wildcards, `%` and `_` are literal (`LIKE 'Q*'` matches all 7 subjects, `LIKE 'Q%'` only "Q%"). `ALIKE` through DAO and `LIKE` through ADO use the ANSI grammar (`%`, `_`). The grammar is selected by the operator/route, not by the pattern.
**Conditions.** DAO and ADO (Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0), ACCDB, default sort.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-01-wildcard-modes/r-expr-1.pat-01-wildcard-modes.8a80744779.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/b-ado/r-sql-1.b-ado.7c2ddbebd8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-wildcard-forms/r-sql-13b.like-wildcard-forms.72ed92cece.a1.json`.
**Open.** The database ANSI-query option and module Option Compare need Access (see status T).

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-02, PAT-04, PAT-05: bracket classes and malformed patterns
**Claim.** `LIKE` and `ALIKE` agree on classes: `[]` matches only the empty string, `[[]` matches "[", `[[]]` "[]", `[!a]` excludes, while `[!]` and `[^]` are literal (`[^a-c]` is the same set as `[a-c]`; `^` is not a negation marker). `[C-A]` and the malformed `[`, `[a`, `[a-`, `a[`, `[z-a]`, `[*`, `[#` fail with "Invalid pattern string" (0x800A005D / error 93) but only for rows where the engine reaches the fault: `q[` fails for subjects "qx" and "qabc" and not for "x", "q", "" or "yq"; `xq[` fails only for "xqx"; the constant `'x' LIKE 'q['` returns 0.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-02-bracket-classes/r-expr-1.pat-02-bracket-classes.1040bfba7a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-03-ranges/r-expr-1.pat-03-ranges.c90e050e13.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-04-05-structural-diagnostics/r-expr-1.pat-04-05-structural-diagnostics.7cb5b6d540.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-bracket-classes/r-sql-13b.like-bracket-classes.ced9bd70e4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-malformed-patterns/r-sql-13b.like-malformed-patterns.d5808645fc.a1.json`.
**Open.** ANSI-92 counterparts through ADO are covered only for PAT-01.

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-06: Null operands and row patterns
**Claim.** `Null LIKE 'q['` is Null with no error (the invalid pattern is not visited); `'' LIKE '*'` is -1 and `'' LIKE '?'` is 0; undeclared parameters holding Empty or "" arrive as Null. `t LIKE t` over a table without bracket rows matches every non-Null row and is Null for a Null row; `CStr(dt) LIKE` over a Null row fails 94 "Invalid use of Null".
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-06-null-and-operand-order/r-expr-1.pat-06-null-and-operand-order.30f6a5a062.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/like-clean-operands/r-fix-expr-sql.like-clean-operands.713010bb35.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-null-pattern-edges/r-sql-13b.like-null-pattern-edges.891651cea6.a1.json`.

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-07, PAT-08, PAT-09, PAT-18: letters, expansions and composition
**Claim.** LIKE treats "æ" as one unit (matches `?`, not `??`, `a?` or `ae`), while `=` and sort treat "æ" = "ae" and "ß" = "ss"; `[æa]e` does not match "æ" and `[æa]?` matches "ae". A decomposed e+U+0301 equals precomposed é under `=` and `StrComp(...,1)` (Len 2 against 1) but `LIKE 'é'` does not match it; dotted/dotless I equal neither "i" nor "I"; half-width ｱ equals ア. Under a traditional Spanish database sort `S > 'cz'` is true for "ch" and ORDER BY places "ch" after "cz"; `?`, `??`, `c?` give identical results in General and Spanish.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB created with the General (0x0409) and the Spanish sort.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-07-08-expansion-and-classes/r-expr-1.pat-07-08-expansion-and-classes.e0b7df7a06.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-09-contraction/r-expr-1.pat-09-contraction.604d219d80.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-18-composition-and-folding/r-expr-1.pat-18-composition-and-folding.ac038a43c0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/like-collation-ranges/r-sql-13b.like-collation-ranges.4bde1411b7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/str-case-folding/r-expr-1b.str-case-folding.17fdcba221.a1.json`.
**Open.** Binary/Text module comparison profiles need VBA; Spanish `[a-ä]` style ranges.

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-10, PAT-13: UTF-16 units and digit membership
**Claim.** `?` consumes one UTF-16 code unit: a surrogate pair needs `??`, and lone U+D800 and U+DC00 each match `?` (Len 1). `#` matches ASCII digits and full-width U+FF11 but not Arabic-Indic U+0661; `[0-9]` matches only ASCII digits.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-10-13-character-boundaries/r-expr-1.pat-10-13-character-boundaries.6a181bed64.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/str-len-lenb-forms/r-expr-1b.str-len-lenb-forms.3150f4cb9d.a1.json`.

### R-EXPR-1 PAT-12, PAT-14: literal shape
**Claim.** `'A ' = 'A'` is -1 (trailing space trimmed in `=`), `StrComp('A ','A')` is 1, `LIKE 'A'` does not match "A " and neither NBSP nor a tab is trimmed; `LIKE 'MN'` matches only the whole subject, case-insensitively.
**Records.** `evidence/r-expr-1/pat-12-14-17-literal-shape/r-expr-1.pat-12-14-17-literal-shape.c4c2b436d3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/str-stored-where-equality/r-def-expr-sql.str-stored-where-equality.f483444341.a1.json`.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-B: source syntax, identifiers and parameters
**Claim.** Bracketed identifiers work (`[Order]`; unbracketed `Order` also opens); backtick quoting and `[x]]y]` fail 3141/0x800A0C45. An unresolved name becomes an implicit Text parameter (3061 "Too few parameters" at OpenRecordset; `Parameters` lists it; after assignment it reads back as Text); an ambiguous name fails 3079. `PARAMETERS a Long, a Text` fails at `Parameters.Count` (3060). Unparenthesised join chains fail 3075/0x800A0C03; `(L LEFT JOIN R) LEFT JOIN RX` works while mixed LEFT/INNER nesting fails 3296; FULL/CROSS/NATURAL/bare JOIN, `JOIN ... USING`, `INNER JOIN` without ON are syntax errors; Memo = Memo join fails 3118; Single = Double and Currency = Double joins work. Output names: `SELECT *` over a join keeps names, same-named aliased columns give "a.id","b.id", expressions get Expr1000...; an empty result keeps typed Fields. A saved QueryDef's SQL comes back re-rendered with a trailing `;`. Statement text limits: 64001 characters accepted, 65535 refused (3035) through OpenRecordset and CreateQueryDef; 50 ANDs accepted, 98 give 3072, 100 give 3360; parenthesis depth 16 accepted, 32 gives 3075; 255 output columns accepted, 256 give 3190; 255 parameters accepted; 32 comma-joined tables accepted, 33 give 3360; 50 SELECT keywords accepted, 51 give 3360.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB (limits also MDB for some probes); ANSI-89 syntax.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-1/b-brackets/r-sql-1.b-brackets.50f5ba938f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/b-name-resolution/r-sql-1.b-name-resolution.8df84d0c53.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/b-parameters/r-sql-1.b-parameters.f46d9b1572.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/b-join-nesting/r-sql-1.b-join-nesting.1d7d6cf711.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/b-join-bound-types/r-sql-1.b-join-bound-types.2530f40599.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/b-output-names/r-sql-1.b-output-names.032255cfb0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/b-limit-length/r-sql-1.b-limit-length.d0e318ce91.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/b-limit-and-nesting/r-sql-1.b-limit-and-nesting.e73502f8c6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/join-keyword-forms/r-sql-13b.join-keyword-forms.65a149c2d7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/join-nesting-four-tables/r-sql-13b.join-nesting-four-tables.6a467dc80b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/select-limit-columns/r-sql-13b.select-limit-columns.30ed344e93.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/sub-nesting-depth/r-sql-13b.sub-nesting-depth.1bfa9bd56c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/join-limit-comma-tables/r-fix-expr-sql.join-limit-comma-tables.570f379a7b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/sql-text-framing-forms/r-def-expr-sql.sql-text-framing-forms.851f4c3811.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/sql-select-modifier-order/r-def-expr-sql.sql-select-modifier-order.91a29d217e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/sql-foreign-identifier-quoting/r-def-expr-sql.sql-foreign-identifier-quoting.3c65830983.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, limits).** AND chains of 60, 70, 80, 90 and 97 conjuncts open (98 failed earlier with 3072 in `r-sql-1/b-limit-and-nesting`; whether its "98 ANDs" and 97 conjuncts count alike was not checked); parenthesis depth 20, 24 and 28 opens and depth 31 fails 3075 "Expression too complex"; statement text of 64500 and 65000 UTF-16 units opens and 65534 fails 3035 "System resource exceeded" through OpenRecordset and through CreateQueryDef. So the limits lie between 28 and 31 (parentheses) and between 65000 and 65534 (length).
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/sqlb-limit-chains/r-q-expr-sql.sqlb-limit-chains.3fe5e6c3b5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/sqlb-limit-length/r-q-expr-sql.sqlb-limit-length.cd25787ee9.a1.json`.
**Open.** Exact thresholds (depth 29 and 30, length 65001 to 65533, AND 97/98 with one counting); the 32-table nested INNER JOIN gives 3072 (limit, or that nest form's own answer?); Access query opening and external-file IN need Access or a path in SQL text (T).

### R-SQL-1 SQL-J: outer joins and ON predicates
**Claim.** `LEFT JOIN ... WHERE R.qty > 0` returns the single matching pair; every ON clause with a non-column-equality conjunct (`ON a = b AND h = 'p'`, `ON a = 1`, `ON ... AND qty>0`, `ON True`) is refused as a whole query with 3296, so ON-versus-WHERE placement cannot be compared; `ON a = b OR ...`, `<`, `<>`, `+`, `IN (...)` are accepted. A Null key never matches and is Null-extended once; a left row with several matches gets all of them; the comma join iterates the first listed table fastest.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-1/j-where-vs-on/r-sql-1.j-where-vs-on.43d308a986.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/j-on-variants/r-sql-1.j-on-variants.df5ed6b170.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/j-null-keys-dups/r-sql-1.j-null-keys-dups.695a04b8f5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/join-outer-null-keys/r-sql-13b.join-outer-null-keys.ca890e4b39.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/join-on-predicate-forms/r-sql-13b.join-on-predicate-forms.aaf58287b0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/join-cross-forms/r-sql-13b.join-cross-forms.445d60f12e.a1.json`.
**Open.** The whole-query refusal of `JOIN ... AND` is unexplained. Interleaved pulls and plan comparison are not asked.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-EQ-01, SQL-EQ-02, SQL-EQ-03, SQL-KEY-01, SQL-KEY-02, SQL-KEY-03: grouping and DISTINCT equality
**Claim.** GROUP BY and DISTINCT over Text merge values that compare equal under the database sort: "abc", "abc ", "ABC", "Abc" form one group, "ae" and "æ" one group, "ß" and "ss" one; " abc", "äbc", `abc\0x` and `abc\0y` and 55-character prefixes stay separate; Null and "" stay separate; Null is one group sorted first. ACCDB and MDB give identical groups; a join `a.t = b.t` pairs the same classes. GROUP BY over Memo works (compared on the first 255 characters; values differing only after 255 merge), DISTINCT over Memo, OLE or Binary is refused (3163, 3114). Numerics: Double -0 and 0 are separate groups; 0.1 as Double and Currency merge; BIGINT 2^53 and 2^53+1 stay distinct alone and merge after UNION with Double. A GROUP BY or DISTINCT over a failing conversion fails at OpenRecordset (3464).
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB and MDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-1/eq-distinct-text/r-sql-1.eq-distinct-text.63e61077f3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/eq-distinct-text-mdb/r-sql-1.eq-distinct-text-mdb.4c4c8a3008.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/eq-cross-type/r-sql-1.eq-cross-type.6be1a0daf9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/eq-numeric-domains/r-sql-1.eq-numeric-domains.86439dd58d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/eq-distinct-error/r-sql-1.eq-distinct-error.3aba2117fa.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/group-null-keys/r-sql-13b.group-null-keys.2b3167e7ae.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/agg-min-max-text/r-sql-13b.agg-min-max-text.3c20621bb8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/memo-distinct-scope/r-sql-13b.memo-distinct-scope.0fdf906886.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/eq-decimal-bigint-groups/r-fix-expr-sql.eq-decimal-bigint-groups.c56046f9b8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-groupby-text-representative/r-par-1.order-groupby-text-representative.9175419765.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, representative).** The spelling shown for a group of values that compare equal is the first row's in scan order: Eq1 inserts 'ae', U+00E6, 'AE' and shows "ae" for DISTINCT, GROUP BY (count 3), Min, Max, First and UNION; Eq2 inserts U+00E6, 'ae', 'AE' and shows U+00E6 for the same statements; an index on t (Eq3) does not change it; Last is "AE"; ORDER BY t lists the three tied rows in descending id (3, 2, 1).
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/eq-repr-order/r-q-expr-sql.eq-repr-order.681e982a07.a1.json`.
**Open.** The earlier records showed U+00E6 for DISTINCT and "ae" for GROUP BY on another fixture; that difference did not reproduce here and was not re-examined. Hash and partition agreement is CalcDb's.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-AGG: empty, Null, Count, result types, singletons
**Claim.** With no GROUP BY an aggregate over an empty or all-Null input returns one row: Count(*) 0, Count(x) 0, Sum/Avg/Min/Max/First/Last Null; with GROUP BY and no rows there are no rows; `HAVING Count(*)=0` returns (0, Null). `Count(*)` counts rows, `Count(d)` non-Null, `Count(Null)` is 0 and `Count('A & B')` counts rows; Count is VT_I4. Sum and Avg of Byte/Integer/Long/Single/Date are Double (no overflow below 1E308); Sum(Currency) is Currency and overflows at open (error 6) when a group exceeds the Currency range; `Sum(2.5)` and Sum/Avg/Min/Max of a Decimal column are Decimal; Min/Max keep the source type (Byte becomes Long); Sum(Yes/No) is -n. Var and StDev of one value are Null, VarP and StDevP are 0; none are Null for two values ((1,3): Var 2, VarP 1, StDev 1.4142135623730951); statistics are Double for every input type. `First` and `Last` return Null rows (they do not skip them). `Min/Max/Sum(Memo)` fail 3115, `Count(DISTINCT x)` is a syntax error. The same aggregate text may appear in SELECT, HAVING and ORDER BY.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB; Decimal columns made by ADO DDL; GROUP BY results read Precision/Scale (28/20, 22/4), ungrouped aggregates cannot (3021).
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-1/agg-empty/r-sql-1.agg-empty.f77ed3cf54.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/agg-count/r-sql-1.agg-count.2a6bfd656d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/agg-result-type/r-sql-1.agg-result-type.f98de5fd4d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/agg-arg-kinds/r-sql-1.agg-arg-kinds.439ac83cbf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/agg-single/r-sql-1.agg-single.f85311d358.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/agg-first-last/r-sql-1.agg-first-last.9d008bdb10.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/agg-occur/r-sql-1.agg-occur.50068df1e2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/agg-sum-boundary/r-sql-13b.agg-sum-boundary.d88bac0645.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/agg-stat-argument-types/r-sql-13b.agg-stat-argument-types.ee4ed8dd0f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/agg-yesno-memo-guid/r-sql-13b.agg-yesno-memo-guid.dc2357037f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/agg-distinct-forms/r-sql-13b.agg-distinct-forms.b8a4971273.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/agg-result-type-decimal/r-fix-expr-sql.agg-result-type-decimal.e3851e0c91.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/agg-stat-argument-types-renamed/r-fix-expr-sql.agg-stat-argument-types-renamed.9bec1c3cc6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/agg-sum-boundary-per-group/r-fix-expr-sql.agg-sum-boundary-per-group.208b46ede7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-double-null-mixed/r-par-1.fold-double-null-mixed.184d3eec19.a1.json`.

**Open.** Count overflow needs more than 2^31 rows; Precision and Scale of an ungrouped aggregate cannot be read (3021). First and Last follow the feed (scan) order of the access path (see the Double fold order note). The variance recurrence is in its own note.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-UNION-01..04: arms
**Claim.** Output names come from the first arm; arity mismatch fails 3307 at open; Long with Text gives Text(255), Date with Double gives Text ("1899/12/31"), the numeric ladder widens b to i to l to d and Currency stays Currency (t5); Decimal-first arm gives Decimal (Precision 22, Scale 4), Currency-first gives Currency, also through ADO. `UNION` removes duplicates under the text sort ('a' and 'A', 'a' and 'a ' collapse) and Null sorts first; `CSng(0.1)` and `CDbl(0.1)` stay two rows. Tokens are kept in the saved SQL: `A UNION B UNION ALL C` returns two rows and `A UNION ALL B UNION C` one. ORDER BY may name only the first arm's alias or position; `UNION DISTINCT`, `CORRESPONDING`, `INTERSECT`, `EXCEPT` are refused; `IN (... UNION ...)` is refused (3787); 50 arms are accepted. A failing conversion in an arm fails at open for UNION (3464) but for UNION ALL it opens and the bad row raises 3071 on MoveNext then reads as Null and iteration continues.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-1/union-binding/r-sql-1.union-binding.968666d2bf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/union-types/r-sql-1.union-types.319d8d2279.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/union-mixed-tokens/r-sql-1.union-mixed-tokens.1112a9592f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/union-error-order/r-sql-1.union-error-order.706a77244a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/union-all-numeric-ladder/r-sql-13b.union-all-numeric-ladder.dc860cdbc9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/union-dedup-cross-type/r-sql-13b.union-dedup-cross-type.6e6a279cbe.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/union-order-by-forms/r-sql-13b.union-order-by-forms.41938a9671.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/union-syntax-forms/r-sql-13b.union-syntax-forms.4aebdb5d51.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/union-arm-count/r-sql-13b.union-arm-count.4cc8acfe76.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/union-types-decimal/r-fix-expr-sql.union-types-decimal.fdd2696d83.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, Null first arm).** `SELECT Null AS v FROM One UNION SELECT col FROM Ut` (distinct UNION) with a later arm of type Long, Double, Date, Currency or Yes/No returns a Text(255) column whose values are the raw bytes of the typed value (Long 7 gives U+0007 U+0000, Currency 1.5 gives U+3A98 U+0000 U+0000 U+0000, Double and Date their eight raw bytes). With UNION ALL the type is kept (Long 4, Double, Date 8, Currency 5, Yes/No 1) although Field.Size reads 510 for the Null-first column (4 or 8 when the typed arm is first). With the typed arm first, UNION and UNION ALL keep the type; a Text later arm stays Text in all orders.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/union-null-first/r-q-expr-sql.union-null-first.79a485d401.a1.json`.
**Open.** Accepted effects in an earlier arm need a host route. The Yes/No rows displayed as empty text, so that case does not show the bytes.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-SUB-01, SQL-SUB-02: scalar subqueries and Null folds
**Claim.** A scalar subquery with 0 rows is Null; with 2 rows it fails 3354 at open; with 2 columns 3306. Over an empty set `IN` is 0, `NOT IN` is -1, `> ALL` is -1 even for a Null outer value; over a set containing Null `NOT IN` is Null for non-matching values and 0 for matches; `a NOT IN (1, Null)` returns only the non-1 non-Null rows; `= ALL` and `<> ALL` over an empty child return every row including Null; `= ANY` over an empty child returns none. `IN (1,'2')` opens and fails on MoveNext (3071) after the first row. EXISTS is I2 -1/0.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-1/sub-scalar/r-sql-1.sub-scalar.f063a4852e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-1/sub-in-quantified/r-sql-1.sub-in-quantified.e0c1fcc3df.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/sub-in-list-null-forms/r-sql-13b.sub-in-list-null-forms.7d6e063de7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/sub-quantified-operators/r-sql-13b.sub-quantified-operators.3faceda688.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/sub-exists-vs-in-null/r-sql-13b.sub-exists-vs-in-null.361a54ca60.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/sub-correlated-select-list/r-sql-13b.sub-correlated-select-list.440d4f7149.a1.json`.
**Open.** Correlation precedence and EXISTS shapes are in the SQL-SUB-03, SQL-SUB-04 note.

### R-SQL-3 SQL-TOP-01..04: TOP
**Claim.** TOP keeps every row tied at the cutoff (`TOP 3 ... ORDER BY K,T` returned 5 rows; TOP 1/5/36 gave 36 tied rows over 256 rows). Admission: `TOP +2`, `&H2`, `2E0`, `2.0`, 2147483647 are accepted, 0, -1, `TOP 0 PERCENT`, >100 PERCENT, `-0`, expressions, parameters and strings are refused (3141, parameters at CreateQueryDef); `TOP 11` over 10 rows returns 10; `TOP 2.5` returns 2. PERCENT returns the ceiling of n% of the row count (10 rows: 15 gives 2, 25 gives 3, 2.5 gives 1; 9 rows: 11 gives 1, 12 gives 2, 34 gives 4, 99 gives 9), ties kept. Null sorts first ascending. Without ORDER BY TOP n returns the first n rows in scan order. A failing sort key fails at open; a failing projection beyond the cutoff raises nothing, a failing row inside the cutoff raises on the Value read.
**Conditions.** DAO, ACCDB and MDB (ties on MDB identical).
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-2-3/top-arguments/r-sql-2-3.top-arguments.d9074eabd8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/top-ties-order/r-sql-2-3.top-ties-order.98f67f5e07.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/top-demand/r-sql-2-3.top-demand.a543dfd216.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/top-expression-forms/r-sql-13b.top-expression-forms.1ffa7c03ff.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/top-parameter-forms/r-sql-13b.top-parameter-forms.df1829c1f7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/top-percent-counts/r-sql-13b.top-percent-counts.d558f3ccec.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-top-n-ties-asc/r-par-1.order-top-n-ties-asc.f00129dc89.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-top-n-ties-asc-mdb/r-par-1.order-top-n-ties-asc-mdb.890f172449.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-top-n-ties-desc/r-par-1.order-top-n-ties-desc.8116a2142c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-top-percent-ties/r-par-1.order-top-percent-ties.2c388425b2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-top-without-order-by/r-par-1.order-top-without-order-by.a0aad8ada5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/err-top-stops-before-bad-row/r-par-1.err-top-stops-before-bad-row.db1df3b225.a1.json`.
**Open.** Order of ties inside the kept group is deterministic but unexplained; spill is CalcDb's.

### R-SQL-3 SQL-DR-01..05: DISTINCTROW
**Claim.** DISTINCTROW deduplicates by source-row identity of every contributing table, not by projected values or whole-row value equality. `DISTINCTROW A.Label` over A (a1 and a2 identical in every field, a3 differing in an unprojected field) joined to B returned 3 rows (plain 6, DISTINCT 1); `B.Tag` alone returned 2 (the fan-out of B collapsed); projecting both families gives 6; a constant projection gives 6, `UCase(A.Label)` 3; a saved query boundary gives 6; a LEFT JOIN keeps an all-Null row. A single-table DISTINCTROW never merges equal rows (Memo/Binary included, 4 rows for 4). `SELECT DISTINCTROW A.Label, A.Hidden ... JOIN B` is updatable: Edit changed one occurrence and Delete removed one physical row. DISTINCT over Memo fails 3163 and Binary 3114.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-2-3/dr-whole-row/r-sql-2-3.dr-whole-row.40add761b9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/dr-participation/r-sql-2-3.dr-participation.6f14df4458.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/dr-calc-saved-outer/r-sql-2-3.dr-calc-saved-outer.9a9ae182d9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/dr-updatable/r-sql-2-3.dr-updatable.f2a07c3654.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/dr-long-values/r-sql-2-3.dr-long-values.802f6dc088.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/agg-distinct-forms/r-sql-13b.agg-distinct-forms.b8a4971273.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-distinct-without-order-by/r-par-1.order-distinct-without-order-by.4a7aac4372.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/order-distinct-full-read/r-fix-expr-sql.order-distinct-full-read.0d563de4a9.a1.json`.
**Open.** The refused provisional choice (whole logical row, 2 classes) is a CalcDb design finding. A direct test of unprojected Memo content is not discriminating because physical identity already separates the rows.

### R-SQL-3 SQL-PIVOT-01..03, -05, -07: crosstab
**Claim.** Fixed IN headings keep list order (`IN ('c','b','a','zz')` publishes Grp, c, b, a, zz); a duplicate or equivalent value in IN ('A','a') fails 0x800A0C20; dynamic headings are the distinct values, Null first as `<>`, numeric headings sort numerically and text as text, 'a' and 'A' merge, a long heading is cut to 64 characters, `.`, `!`, `]` become `_`, `3.0` becomes "3_0", dates render "2024/01/02". `IN ('')` and `IN (Null)` both fill `<>`; a declared parameter in IN is a literal heading. Empty input: dynamic gives 1 field and 0 rows, fixed gives its headings with 0 rows; outside-list-only still returns every row group with Null cells. A missing cell is Null for every value function; a seen cell of all-Null values gives Count(x) 0 and Null Sum/Avg/First. The fixed crosstab's QueryDef.Fields has its headings before any open; a dynamic one has 0 fields until OpenRecordset and then keeps its schema through MoveNext and MoveLast and after an insert. Heading limit: 254 headings (255 fields) accepted, 255 headings fail "Too many crosstab column headers" (0x800A0C85); `Requery` on a crosstab dynaset or snapshot fails (3251/0x800A0CB3).
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-headings/r-sql-2-3.pivot-headings.68d1c68c44.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/pivot-headings-clean/r-fix-expr-sql.pivot-headings-clean.77f1d9ddd8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-empty-null/r-sql-2-3.pivot-empty-null.382879f2a4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-cells/r-sql-2-3.pivot-cells.7e6eb2df0a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-schema-lifetime/r-sql-2-3.pivot-schema-lifetime.27ebaee52e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/pivot-dynaset-requery/r-fix-expr-sql.pivot-dynaset-requery.5b56a896c6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/pivot-limit/r-sql-2-3.pivot-limit.8c88c64eb9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/pivot-clause-forms/r-sql-13b.pivot-clause-forms.2c2071ebfb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/pivot-heading-sort-order/r-sql-13b.pivot-heading-sort-order.77fabece6d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/pivot-in-list-forms/r-sql-13b.pivot-in-list-forms.b2ca805610.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/pivot-parameter-positions/r-sql-13b.pivot-parameter-positions.b3329bc546.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, rendered names).** Numeric headings 1, 1.5, 10, -1 sort numerically and render `-1`, `1`, `1_5`, `10` (rows with 1 and 1 sum to 6); text headings '1', '1.0', '01' render "1", "1_0", "01" and stay distinct (with a fixed IN list in written order, cells 1, 2, 3); 'a' and 'A' merge (11). A leading-space ' 1' and '1' render to one column "1" that holds 4, the ' 1' row's value, not the sum 5: two distinct keys with one rendered name collapse silently to a single column.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/pivot-typed-collisions/r-q-expr-sql.pivot-typed-collisions.68ca3bd422.a1.json`.
**Open.** The mechanism of the ' 1' collision (overwrite or first-name wins); a typed number against a text with the same rendering cannot share one pivot column; locale rendering needs a settings change (T); `PIVOT IN (1.0, 1)` returning zero rows is unexplained.

### R-SQL-3 SQL-META (parameters): declaration, types and value adaptation
**Claim.** Declared types map as Bit 1, Byte 2, Short 3, Long 4 (also Integer, Int), Currency 5, IEEESingle/Single/Real 6, IEEEDouble/Double/Float/Numeric 7, DateTime 8, Binary 9, Text/Char/VarChar/Value 10, LongBinary 11, LongText 12, Guid 15, BigInt 19; `Decimal`, `Memo`, `Counter`, `Boolean`, `Variant`, `TinyInt` are refused (3139). A trailing `;` is required after PARAMETERS; two PARAMETERS clauses fail 3129; duplicate names are accepted at CreateQueryDef and fail at `Parameters.Count` (3060); 64- and 65-character names open. A parameter in an ON clause is refused (3296), every other position opens. Assigned values adapt: Long and Short round half to even (2.5 gives 2, 2.6 gives 3, 3.5 gives 4, -2.5 gives -2), Byte rejects -2.5, 255.5, 300 and text (3421), Bit becomes -1 for any non-Null number, DateTime rejects "abc" and "5" (3421) but takes I4 70000, Text keeps text longer than its declared length (10 and 26 characters read in full), Currency/Single/Double/DateTime keep the value; a rejected assignment leaves the old value; an unassigned parameter fails 3061 at OpenRecordset; Null makes `x = p` Null and `p Is Null` -1. `QueryDef.Fields` of a saved parameter query is readable before assignment.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-declaration-syntax/r-sql-13b.params-declaration-syntax.d11177e387.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-declared-type-names/r-sql-13b.params-declared-type-names.def4e28133.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-positions/r-sql-13b.params-positions.5c6047d0f4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-count-limit/r-sql-13b.params-count-limit.dd1f5a3b5e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/params-values-rounding-and-length/r-fix-expr-sql.params-values-rounding-and-length.99dc9925f0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/parameters-values-set/r-fix-expr-sql.parameters-values-set.55c1458fde.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/nz-free-forms/r-fix-expr-sql.nz-free-forms.4105e37dd6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/params-saved/r-sql-2-3.params-saved.a78be0cf53.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/params-implicit-temp/r-sql-2-3.params-implicit-temp.38ef68ab0e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/params-value-readback/r-sql-13b.params-value-readback.1f3eed4e11.a1.json`.
**Open.** `Parameters.Count` fails when 255 or more parameters are all used (unexplained). Parameter lifecycle on a held recordset is in the EXPR-BIND note.

### R-SQL-2 ACT-UPD-01, ACT-UPD-03, ACT-UPD-04, ACT-INS-02, ACT-INS-03: assignment views, joined targets, counts, INSERT SELECT
**Claim.** UPDATE assignments read the original row: `SET V=W, W=V` swaps in either textual order and `V=W+1, W=V+1, S=V&'-'&W` uses the old values; two assignments to one field fail 3063 with nothing changed; `SET N=(SELECT...)` and `SET ID=ID+1` (collision) are refused. A join UPDATE counts joined occurrences (P1 matched by two children counts 3), `SET P.Name=C.Tag` leaves the last child's value, DISTINCTROW counts 2, a DISTINCT saved query as target fails 3073, LEFT JOIN with the outer side as target fails 3058, a two-target SET changes both tables, dbConsistent/dbInconsistent both succeed. RecordsAffected counts no-op assignments (`SET V=V` gives 4), is not counted for cascades, is set on the QueryDef that executed and `Database.RecordsAffected` stays 0 until `Database.Execute` runs. INSERT SELECT from the target table is finite (4 rows gives 8 rows; appended rows do not re-enter), a SELECT without a column list maps by name, a short select fails 3314, one extra value 3346; omitted column gets its default (5) and explicit Null stays Null; insertion through an updatable saved query or a join succeeds.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-upd-assign/r-sql-2-3.act-upd-assign.ac3d38f712.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/update-assignment-reseeded/r-fix-expr-sql.update-assignment-reseeded.92fbe9f025.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-upd-join/r-sql-2-3.act-upd-join.9e423c7a63.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-records-affected/r-sql-2-3.act-records-affected.53ac1b81d4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-cascade/r-sql-2-3.act-cascade.9c205eff72.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-qdef-routes/r-sql-2-3.act-qdef-routes.297136c51e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-ins-self/r-sql-2-3.act-ins-self.29656dc32f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-ins-mapping/r-sql-2-3.act-ins-mapping.9e73b54376.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-ins-defaults/r-sql-2-3.act-ins-defaults.f84f17d4f5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-expr-sql/sql-insert-values-forms/r-def-expr-sql.sql-insert-values-forms.7ffaec6fa3.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, joined target layout).** A joined `UPDATE PJ INNER JOIN Cx ON PJ.ID = Cx.PID SET PJ.Name = Cx.Tag` with two children leaves the value of the last child in scan (insertion) order: children inserted 10 then 11 leave "c11", inserted 11 then 10 leave "c10", with and without an index on the foreign key; RecordsAffected is 2 each time.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/act-upd-join-layout/r-q-expr-sql.act-upd-join-layout.105930ad58.a1.json`.
**Open.** Joined target with an effectful expression needs a host route; stale `RecordsAffected` after DDL; reentry needs a host route.

### R-SQL-2 SQL-CONTINUE: row failure without dbFailOnError
**Claim.** Without dbFailOnError a failing candidate is skipped without an error and later candidates continue, for unique (3022), validation (3316), relationship (3200/3201), required (3314) and conversion (3464) failures; the rows that changed are counted in RecordsAffected and Errors stays unchanged (a single-row failing INSERT gives RecordsAffected 0 and no error). A locked row (second Workspace pessimistic Edit) gives success with RecordsAffected 0; with dbFailOnError 3188. A conversion failure stores Null for that row and counts it not. These hold on MDB for the unique and lock cases.
**Conditions.** DAO Database.Execute and QueryDef.Execute, ACCDB and MDB.
**Records.** `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-fail-unique/r-sql-2-3.act-fail-unique.8d251e639e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/mdb-fail-unique/r-sql-2-3.mdb-fail-unique.29f2e5ac58.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-fail-validation/r-sql-2-3.act-fail-validation.6ad741c4ef.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fail-relationship-update-isolated/r-fix-expr-sql.fail-relationship-update-isolated.1f9c1bdf98.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fail-insert-required-cross-table/r-fix-expr-sql.fail-insert-required-cross-table.010320f5e9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-fail-conversion/r-sql-2-3.act-fail-conversion.0dcbbf3a73.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/act-lock-blocked/r-sql-2-3.act-lock-blocked.c1f4d15854.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-2-3/mdb-lock-blocked/r-sql-2-3.mdb-lock-blocked.833f67bbeb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-expr-1b/route-action-query-expressions/r-expr-1b.route-action-query-expressions.92a71c40cb.a1.json`.

**Claim (follow-up, locks).** A pessimistic Edit by a second Workspace on any one row of a 3-row table (ID 2, ID 1 or ID 3), or on all three, blocks the whole UPDATE: without dbFailOnError the call succeeds with RecordsAffected 0 and no row changes, including the unblocked rows; with dbFailOnError it fails 3188 with no row changed. A successful no-flag Execute neither clears nor adds to Errors (the marker's 3376 entry is still the only one). The lock therefore covers a page (all rows here share one), not a row.
**Records (follow-up).** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/cont-blocked-positions/r-q-expr-sql.cont-blocked-positions.ba95f08bc3.a1.json`.

**Claim (round 2, other pages).** In Big (65,536 rows, 18 rows per page, ID order = physical order) a pessimistic Edit by a second Workspace on one row blocks only the rows of its page. Without dbFailOnError the UPDATE succeeds, skips the locked page and changes every other row: blocking ID 0 leaves IDs 0..17 unchanged (RecordsAffected 65518), blocking ID 30000 leaves IDs 29988..30005 unchanged (65518), blocking ID 65535 leaves IDs 65520..65535 unchanged (65520); Errors is unchanged. With dbFailOnError the statement stops at the first locked page with 3188 and RecordsAffected 0, and the rows before it stay changed: blocking ID 0 changes no row, blocking ID 30000 changes IDs 0..29987 (Sum(V) 29988), blocking ID 65535 changes IDs 0..65519.
**Records (round 2).** `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/cont-multipage-noflag/r-q2-expr-sql.cont-multipage-noflag.b76c53bc7b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/cont-multipage-flag/r-q2-expr-sql.cont-multipage-flag.ae9313e97a.a1.json`.
**Open.** Several locked pages in one statement; first/last/all blocked on single-page tables is the earlier note.

### R-PAR-1 PAR-FOLD: Currency, Decimal and Single accumulation
**Claim.** Sum and Avg over Currency overflow at OpenRecordset (error 6) whenever the exact running total leaves the Currency range, even if the final sum would be in range (g1, g2, g4..g6, g11, g12), and succeed at the boundary values (g3 922337203685477.5807, g8 0.9999); Var/StDev over Currency overflow in 10 of 12 groups. `Sum(CDbl(c))` of the same group is Double 922337203685477.6. A Decimal column sums as Decimal at full precision: 28 nines + 1 - 28 nines is 1; 28 nines + 1 and a group of 28 nines overflows with 3761; Sum(x*1) stays Decimal; Avg is Decimal with the scale needed (0.3333333333333333333333333333); Var/StDev are Double or overflow (6). Single sums are Double (g3 6.805646932770577E+38, no overflow).
**Records.** `evidence/r-par-1/fold-currency-sum-groups/r-par-1.fold-currency-sum-groups.ac7abebfe6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-currency-avg-minmax/r-par-1.fold-currency-avg-minmax.4b4330c5d6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-currency-var-stdev/r-par-1.fold-currency-var-stdev.decaedc844.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-currency-argument-forms/r-par-1.fold-currency-argument-forms.efd4183936.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-decimal-sum-groups/r-par-1.fold-decimal-sum-groups.5dc68e238f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fold-decimal-28-digits/r-fix-expr-sql.fold-decimal-28-digits.430d58abfd.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fold-decimal-avg-var-forms/r-fix-expr-sql.fold-decimal-avg-var-forms.04deca2ebd.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/fold-single-groups/r-par-1.fold-single-groups.12570a2f84.a1.json`, `evidence/r-sql-13b/agg-sum-boundary/r-sql-13b.agg-sum-boundary.d88bac0645.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/agg-sum-boundary-per-group/r-fix-expr-sql.agg-sum-boundary-per-group.208b46ede7.a1.json`.

**Open.** Whether Currency accumulates as exact 64-bit integers or in a wider intermediate is not distinguished by these records. The Double order is in the Double fold order note.

### R-PAR-1 PAR-ORDER: scan order, grouping order and ties
**Claim.** An unordered table scan returns insertion order, not primary-key order (OT5/OT2/OT3/OT4 identical, filtered scans identical); a dynaset of the table returns primary-key order while snapshot and forward-only return insertion order; GROUP BY and DISTINCT output is ascending by key; UNION returns ascending distinct values and UNION ALL keeps arm order; ORDER BY ties come out in a deterministic order that is not insertion order, identical across tables with different indexes, and it changes after DELETE/INSERT/UPDATE; TOP n and TOP n PERCENT keep the whole tie group (TOP 5 over a join returned 24 rows). The order of join duplicate matches depends on the table pairing and indexes.
**Records.** `evidence/r-par-1/order-scan-storage-order/r-par-1.order-scan-storage-order.7c47b25e2c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-scan-filter-routes/r-par-1.order-scan-filter-routes.83879b1a70.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-recordset-types/r-par-1.order-recordset-types.e232632274.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-groupby-output-order/r-par-1.order-groupby-output-order.b48c25d6be.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-groupby-output-order-mdb/r-par-1.order-groupby-output-order-mdb.ef422953ef.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/order-distinct-full-read/r-fix-expr-sql.order-distinct-full-read.0d563de4a9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-union-without-order-by/r-par-1.order-union-without-order-by.8c1f7f4de4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-ties-order-by-k/r-par-1.order-ties-order-by-k.29d06704d8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-after-delete-insert-update/r-par-1.order-after-delete-insert-update.6d90c07ccb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-par-1/order-join-duplicate-matches/r-par-1.order-join-duplicate-matches.eb3683de21.a1.json`.
**Open.** The tie order itself has no explanation (a sort algorithm artefact); no claim about it beyond "deterministic, not insertion order".

### R-PAR-1 PAR-FOLD, R-SQL-1 SQL-AGG-ORDER, SQL-AGG-FIRST: Double Sum follows the feed order, which an index maintained during insert scrambles
**Claim.** Sum and Avg of Double are a strictly sequential left-to-right accumulation in the order the rows reach the aggregate. In all eleven tables the Sum for group 3 (2^53, 4096 ones, -2^53 by id 8195, 8196..12291, 12292) equals the sum recomputed offline in IEEE doubles from the retained scan order: FL, FLD (index on d only), FLN (primary key only), FLX (no key), FLC (make-table copy) and the three post-load tables (index on g, on d, on both created after the rows exist) read group 3 in id order (8195..12292) and sum 0; FLR (copy inserted ORDER BY id DESC) reads 12292 down to 8195 and sums 4096; FLI (indexes on g and d built before INSERT SELECT) reads group 3 as 9701..10282, 8195..9700, 10283..12292 and sums 584; FLG (index on g only, built before the insert) reads 8343..9506, 8195..8342, 9507..12292 and sums 1164. WHERE g = 3 and GROUP BY g give the same Sum; First and Last of id equal the first and last ids of the retained order (FLI 9701 and 12292); the whole-table scan starts 1..40 in every table except FLR (12292 downward). So an index on g that was maintained during the insert delivers an equal-key run in a rotated order of id runs, an index on g built after the load, an index on d only, no index, a make-table copy and a reversed insert give plain scan order, and the Sum is whatever the sequential sum of that order is. The earlier 1360 for FLI did not repeat: the rebuilt FLI (same recipe) gives 584 with a different rotation, so the rotation point depends on the build, not the recipe.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB, one process; offline sums recomputed in awk (IEEE doubles) from the arrays retained by GetRows.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FL/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FL.afd432826c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLI/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLI.b10577fe82.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLG/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLG.6d952e77cf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLD/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLD.6838b79f00.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLN/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLN.f8d7f77466.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLX/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLX.5a76eac0b6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLR/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLR.be5581ea58.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-scan-FLC/r-q-expr-sql.fold-scan-FLC.6afd4dd7a1.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-post-g/r-q-expr-sql.fold-post-g.3a471b968a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-post-d/r-q-expr-sql.fold-post-d.ea93514e2a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/fold-post-both/r-q-expr-sql.fold-post-both.79ca5ef774.a1.json`; earlier: `evidence/r-par-1/fold-double-large-groups/r-par-1.fold-double-large-groups.b518c9662a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/fold-double-routes-materialized/r-fix-expr-sql.fold-double-routes-materialized.765f09d5d6.a1.json`.

**Claim (round 2, many groups).** The same holds for 4096 groups of [2^53, 1, -2^53]: on FG (primary key only), FGI (index on g built before the insert) and FG with an index on g built after the load, one `GROUP BY g` with `Sum(d)`, `First(id)` and `Last(id)` gives, for every one of the 4096 groups, First = the 2^53 row (id g), Last = the -2^53 row (id 8192 + g) and Sum 0, which is the sequential sum of the id order; single-group reads (g = 1, 151, 1000, 4096) return the ids g, 4096 + g, 8192 + g in that scan order, and the whole-table scan starts at id 1..40. So with many groups the feed order is the scan order in all three layouts; the earlier FG result of 1164 groups summing 1 (groups 151 to 1314) did not reproduce on this rebuild (the rebuild's groups 151, 1000 and 4096 are in id order with Sum 0).
**Records (round 2).** `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/fold-many-FG/r-q2-expr-sql.fold-many-FG.a59a345e6a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/fold-many-FGI/r-q2-expr-sql.fold-many-FGI.2c63a19193.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-expr-sql/fold-many-FGP/r-q2-expr-sql.fold-many-FGP.9e51d6cfa3.a1.json`.
**Open.** The earlier 1164-group result and the earlier 1360 (FLI) are build-dependent layouts (the rebuilt FLI gave 584, the rebuilt FG gave none): why an index on g maintained during insert, or an earlier build of FG, delivers a rotated order is not explained, and no probe made a scrambled many-group layout on demand.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-AGG-NUMERIC: Var, VarP, StDev and StDevP come from single-pass Double sums
**Claim.** ACE computes the variance family from Σx and Σx² accumulated in Double, not from deviations. For 1e9 + (4, 7, 13, 16) (exact sample variance 30) ACE returns Var 170.66666666666666 and VarP 128; for 1e15 + (1, 2, 3) (exact 1) 187649984473770.66 and 125099989649180.44; for 1e8 + (0.1, 0.2, 0.3) (exact 0.01) 2.6666666666666665 and 1.7777777777777777; for 1e16 + (0, 2, 4) (exact 4) 24019198012642644 and 16012798675095096. These magnitudes are reproduced exactly offline in IEEE doubles by (n·Σx² − (Σx)²) / (n·(n−1)) for Var and / n² for VarP; ACE returns them positive although that expression is negative for these inputs (the sign handling is not determined). StDev and StDevP are their square roots (13.063945294843617 and 11.313708498984761 for the first group). 1e150 · (1, 2, 3) gives Var 1.0000000000000006E+300 (exact 1e300) and 1e154 · (1, 2, 3) fails with Overflow (6) at OpenRecordset because the squares leave the Double range although the variance is representable.
**Conditions.** DAO snapshot, ACCDB, Double column, GROUP BY and WHERE g = k.
**Records.** `evidence/r-q-expr-sql/agg-var-recurrence/r-q-expr-sql.agg-var-recurrence.d29f892304.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-expr-sql/agg-numeric-per-group/r-fix-expr-sql.agg-numeric-per-group.4cfc915b42.a1.json`.
**Open.** Sign handling; accumulation order for sums that cancel (the order dependence is in the Double fold order note); Currency, Decimal and Date inputs.

### R-SQL-1 SQL-SUB-03, SQL-SUB-04: correlation names and EXISTS shapes
