### R-ADO-1 ADO-SCHEMA: field metadata, refresh after DDL and schema routes
- **Claim**: ADO publishes ACE field types as: Long 3, Text 202 (size 10/255), Double 5, Currency 6, Date 7, Memo 203 (536870910), Binary 205 / 204, Yes/No 11, Short 2, Byte 17, Single 4, GUID 72, Decimal 131 (precision 38 for DAO-made fields, as declared for DDL columns). Decimal expressions: N, N+1, N+N, N/2, -N are adNumeric 131 (precision 18, scale 4); `N*1.5` is adDouble; Sum(N) precision 28 scale 4; Count(N) adInteger. After `ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` with the table closed, a retained Fields collection reports the old count until Requery (client cursor: 5 after Refresh, 6 after Requery; on a keyset the ALTER itself fails 0x80040E14 "could not lock table" while the recordset is open); a new recordset sees the column. `Connection.OpenSchema(id)` with the restrictions argument omitted succeeds for 16 SchemaEnum values: 4, 5, 6, 8, 9, 10, 12, 16, 19, 20, 22, 23, 27, 28, 30 (67 rows) and 31 (16 rows), all forward-only (RecordCount -1) except those two; 0, 1, 2, 3, 7, 11, 13, 14, 15, 17, 18, 21, 24, 25, 26, 29 and 32 to 41 fail 0x800A0CB3 and 9999 fails 0x800A0BB9.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/schema-ids-no-restrictions/r-q-ado-misc.schema-ids-no-restrictions.f1f79a0408.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/fields-meta-mdb/r-ado-1b.fields-meta-mdb.b93c565d13.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-meta-decimal-expressions/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-meta-decimal-expressions.a7d2aad72e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-closed/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-refresh-ddl-closed.2b8ac47651.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-client-only/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-refresh-ddl-client-only.27000fbbd5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/fields-refresh-ddl-keyset-only/r-fix-wire-ado.fields-refresh-ddl-keyset-only.fdd76b6a40.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-schema/r-str-ado-act.ado-schema.a2208b3039.a1.json`. Discriminator: the earlier "every other id refused" (`evidence/r-oledb-odbc/schema-others/r-oledb-odbc.schema-others.4ef914e3e7.a1.json`) passed scalar restrictions; without them ids 5, 8, 9, 10, 28, 30 and 31 are supported, so that earlier list is superseded.
- **Open**: restriction arrays and restriction positions (array argument: T); SchemaEnum names are from the ADO type library, not tabulated in these records.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-RECORD: Record.Open forms on the ACE provider
- **Claim**: None of the Record sources asked is supported by the ACE provider: `Record.Open("K", cn)` and `Record.Open("SELECT * FROM K", cn)` fail 0x800A0E7D ("The connection cannot be used to perform this operation"), `Record.Open(Command, cn)` and (earlier) `Record.Open(Recordset)` fail 0x800A0CB3, and the Record stays State 0 (its RecordType and ParentURL then fail 0x800A0E78). No URL, file or network source was asked (scope).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/record-open-forms/r-q-ado-misc.record-open-forms.aa2320dcd3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/stream-record-from-row/r-oledb-odbc.stream-record-from-row.a529dfd825.a1.json`.
- **Open**: URL/external namespaces are outside the initial scope.

### R-ADO-1 ADO-STREAM: standalone text and binary streams, files and type switching
- **Claim**: A new text stream is Type 2, Charset "Unicode". `WriteText` of "a b" plus a surrogate pair gives Size 12 (BOM FFFE plus 10 bytes) and Position 12, a following write with an embedded NUL adds 6 bytes (NUL kept), `WriteText(..., adWriteLine)` appends the text plus CRLF (default LineSeparator), and after `LineSeparator = 10` appends LF. `ReadText(adReadLine)` with LineSeparator 10 read to the first LF (the CRLFs are not separators); `SkipLine` at the end is a no-op; `ReadText(-1)` at the end returns "" with EOS true. Changing Charset at Position 0 keeps Size; reading after `Charset = "utf-8"` fails 0x80070057 (as earlier) and after "ascii" returns the raw bytes as characters; `SetEOS` at Position 3 gives Size 3; `CopyTo` converts between charsets (3 ascii-read bytes gave 8 bytes in a Unicode stream, BOM included). Binary stream: `SaveToFile(path, adSaveCreateNotExist)` creates the file, again with 1 fails 0x800A0BBC "Write to file failed", with adSaveCreateOverWrite (2) succeeds; `LoadFromFile` restores the 3 bytes; `ReadText` on a binary stream fails 0x800A0C93; switching a stream with data from binary to text fails 0x800A0C93 at Position 2 and works at Position 0 (reading `410042` as Unicode returns "A"); `LoadFromFile` of the fixture database gives Size 200704 (its file size); a missing file fails 0x800A0BBA "File could not be opened" (Size 0); after `Close`, `Size` and `ReadText` fail 0x800A0E78; `SetEOS` at position 0 gives Size 0.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/stream-text-ops/r-q-ado-misc.stream-text-ops.8475971f2f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/stream-file-ops/r-q-ado-misc.stream-file-ops.17ab489f19.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-stream/r-str-ado-act.ado-stream.87b16fa91d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/stream-appendchunk/r-oledb-odbc.stream-appendchunk.389512d0b0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/stream-getchunk/r-oledb-odbc.stream-getchunk.41c7919f33.a1.json`.
- **Open**: sharing/partial-failure and overlapping source/destination, non-zero-bound arrays (builder).

### R-ADO-1 ADO-PERSIST (part): save, reopen and reconnect through ADTG and XML
- **Claim**: `Recordset.Save` writes ADTG (568 B for 7 rows; 985/1005 B for the 4-row fixture) or XML (2922 B to a Stream; 3430 B to a file) and both reopen (`adCmdFile`, client, batch) with RecordCount, value tags (I4, BSTR, R8, CY, DATE, UI1 array, Null) and, with pending changes, Status 2, `OriginalValue` and the deleted row preserved; a persisted batch reconnects with `ActiveConnection` set by reference and `UpdateBatch` stores the edit, delete and insert; a second `Save` to an existing path fails 0x80030050 in the one-argument and both two-argument forms, `Save(path, 99)` fails 0x800A0BB9, `Save` after Close 0x800A0E78; empty, tiny, zero-filled, "<xml" and BOM-only files fail with the persistence provider's own messages and a missing file 0x80030002. `Provider="MSPersist"` serves persisted files.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-ado-1b/persist-stream-roundtrip/r-ado-1b.persist-stream-roundtrip.1b1422d4ce.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/persist-reconnect-adtg/r-ado-1b.persist-reconnect-adtg.6f02a7cc77.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/persist-reconnect-xml/r-ado-1b.persist-reconnect-xml.9a8c688f98.a1.json`, `evidence/r-ado-1b/persist-malformed-files/r-ado-1b.persist-malformed-files.1a8401c6f2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/persist-save-two-parameter/r-fix-wire-ado.persist-save-two-parameter.c7a3307497.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/ado-persist/r-str-ado-act.ado-persist.4ad2e7d2b5.a1.json`. Discriminator: the original flag-argument defect (`Save(path,0,1)` returned 0x8002000E) is repaired by the two-parameter forms.
- **Open**: ADTG byte conformance against MS-ADTG is W-04 file-byte work (T); truncated-length variants.

### R-ENC-1 ENC-PROFILE: creation routes and credential errors
- **Claim**: `CreateDatabase` succeeds for plain MDB (64) and ACCDB (128), MDB with `dbEncrypt` (66), and with `;pwd=` in the locale string (MDB and ACCDB), and a password can be given afterwards by `NewPassword("", pw)`; ACCDB with `dbEncrypt` (130) always fails 3001/0x800A0BB9 (no file; repeated in four probes). `OpenDatabase` on a password-protected file fails 3031 "Not a valid password" when the password is omitted, empty, wrong, or the right one in a different case, and succeeds with the exact password in either mode; ADO gives 0x80040E4D "Not a valid password" (SQLState 3031) and the open with the password works. Opening unprotected or `dbEncrypt` (no password) files with a wrong or empty password succeeds (9 opens, Errors 0). `DBEngine.Errors` keeps a stale 3031 after a later successful open.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-enc-link/profile-pwd-mdb/r-enc-link.profile-pwd-mdb.a63829033a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/profile-newpwd-accdb/r-enc-link.profile-newpwd-accdb.b89b762f66.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/create-enc-accdb-enc/r-enc-link.create-enc-accdb-enc.7e66c728aa.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/ado-open-pwd-mdb/r-enc-link.ado-open-pwd-mdb.4ffeffe8cc.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/profile-unprotected-existing-roles/r-fix-cat-txn.profile-unprotected-existing-roles.5b8f77af7d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/enc-accdb-compact-encrypt-130/r-fix-cat-txn.enc-accdb-compact-encrypt-130.7e634ca46b.a1.json`.
- **Open**: header discriminators in malformed copies (byte-level, T); encrypted ACCDB through a supported route other than the password route.

### R-ENC-1 ENC-UPDATE: edits through a password-protected file
- **Claim**: With the password in the Connect argument, updates at the start, middle and end (3000-character memos), an insert, a range delete and a bulk insert all succeed on MDB and ACCDB with Errors 0, reopen counts matching (N 64 to 108, memo total 192000 to 330000), file growing 655360 to 1024000 B (MDB) and 749568 to 1118208 B (ACCDB).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-enc-link/update-pwd-mdb/r-enc-link.update-pwd-mdb.7675cf8363.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/update-pwd-accdb/r-enc-link.update-pwd-accdb.a5b498d7f4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/enc-xproc-protected-holder/r-def-cat-txn.enc-xproc-protected-holder.9d0fa36b43.a1.json`.
- **Open**: torn and failed writes (ENC-FAIL, T); full page images (ENC-PAGE, T).


### R-ENC-1 ENC-PASSWORD: set, change, remove and refusals through NewPassword
- **Claim**: On an exclusive read/write open, `NewPassword("", pw)` sets a password on plain MDB and ACCDB, `NewPassword(old, new)` changes it (old password then gives 3031) and `NewPassword(pw, "")` removes it (MDB and ACCDB; the file then opens with no password), each with the file size unchanged and a different hash. On a protected file: `NewPassword` on an exclusive READ-ONLY open (with the password) fails 0x800A0BD3 / 3027 "Cannot update. Database or object is read-only."; on a shared read/write open it fails 0x800A0E25 / 3621 "Cannot change password on a shared open database."; neither changes the password (no password gives 3031, the old one opens, the attempted new one gives 3031). A wrong old password fails 3031 and leaves the file unchanged. Passwords of 1 to 20 characters, including non-ASCII, a space and a double quote, work; a 21-character password or one containing `;` fails 3031 and leaves the file unprotected (`;pwd=semi;colon` as Connect gives 3001). Identical on MDB and ACCDB.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/enc-password-protected-first-accdb/r-q-ado-misc.enc-password-protected-first-accdb.b9e465023b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/enc-password-protected-first-mdb/r-q-ado-misc.enc-password-protected-first-mdb.161c1bec14.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/newpassword-set-mdb/r-enc-link.newpassword-set-mdb.ec1fa4633e.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/newpassword-set-accdb/r-enc-link.newpassword-set-accdb.bb63173477.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/newpassword-remove-mdb/r-enc-link.newpassword-remove-mdb.ef48ab4ac3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/password-wrong-old/r-enc-link.password-wrong-old.8cfd005793.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/enc-password-ladder-accdb/r-def-cat-txn.enc-password-ladder-accdb.189afe2bcf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/enc-password-ladder-mdb/r-def-cat-txn.enc-password-ladder-mdb.604ea7bc93.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/enc-newpassword-shared-and-readonly-opens/r-def-cat-txn.enc-newpassword-shared-and-readonly-opens.c0b3108157.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/enc-newpassword-shared-and-readonly-protected-first/r-fix-cat-txn.enc-newpassword-shared-and-readonly-protected-first.a8acc0959b.a1.json`. Discriminator: protected-first order (the earlier shared/read-only probes had never protected the file, so their refusals could not be told from "already unprotected").
- **Open**: secured-workgroup refusal (NotModelled); the Access UI route; the transaction participation of NewPassword.

### R-ENC-1 ENC-REKEY: CompactDatabase password and encryption options
- **Claim**: `CompactDatabase` with the source password in SrcLocale and `;pwd=new` in DstLocale (version option 128 or 64) produces a destination that opens only with the new password (MDB and ACCDB) and leaves the source unchanged; an explicit empty `;pwd=` in DstLocale (ACCDB, SrcLocale `;pwd=pwk`) produces a destination that opens with no password (and, being unprotected, also with any password string); compacting a protected file with the password only in SrcLocale and no `;pwd=` in DstLocale gives an output that still needs a password. On a plain MDB option 66 gives a destination that opens without a password and 66 with `;pwd=encpw` one that needs it; MDB `dbDecrypt` (68) gives no password. On ACCDB every encrypt/decrypt option asked fails 3001 (0x800A0BB9 "Invalid argument") and creates no file: 128+2, 128+4, 2 alone and 4 alone from a password-protected source, and 130 from a plain source (earlier, repeated in four records).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/enc-rekey-accdb-options/r-q-ado-misc.enc-rekey-accdb-options.504339151d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/rekey-pwd-mdb/r-enc-link.rekey-pwd-mdb.39de42f383.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/rekey-pwd-accdb/r-enc-link.rekey-pwd-accdb.2587772546.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/rekey-options-mdb/r-enc-link.rekey-options-mdb.321baeb62f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/enc-accdb-compact-encrypt-decrypt-options/r-def-cat-txn.enc-accdb-compact-encrypt-decrypt-options.02d7b8108b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/enc-accdb-compact-password-route/r-fix-cat-txn.enc-accdb-compact-password-route.1806fad84a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/options-encrypt-password/r-cplx-rebuild.options-encrypt-password.c22df96ead.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/enc-accdb-compact-encrypt-130/r-fix-cat-txn.enc-accdb-compact-encrypt-130.7e634ca46b.a1.json`.
- **Open**: a host replacement route (T); Access UI encrypt command (T).

### R-ENC-1 ENC-LIFE: held cursor across a password change (MDB and ACCDB)
- **Claim**: On an open exclusive database (MDB and ACCDB), `NewPassword("", pw)` or `NewPassword(old, new)` does not disturb a held dynaset: `MoveNext` reads the next row, an `Execute UPDATE` runs, and a fresh snapshot reads the update; after close the old password (or none) gives 3031 and the new password opens (ACCDB: no password 3031, `pwl` opens with 3 rows). MDB: Bookmark of the held dynaset stays a 4-byte array.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/enc-life-held-cursor-accdb/r-q-ado-misc.enc-life-held-cursor-accdb.abbc04f86c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/life-held-cursor-mdb/r-enc-link.life-held-cursor-mdb.c8bfa73ec8.a1.json`.
- **Open**: host-admission revocation and key-buffer lifetime (T).

### R-LINK-1 LINK-META: link descriptors, live field lists and enumeration without the source
- **Claim**: A persistent link made by `CreateTableDef(name, 0, SourceTableName, Connect)` and `TableDefs.Append` has Attributes 1073741824 (`dbAttachedTable`), 1073872896 with `dbAttachSavePWD` (131072) set before Append; Connect reads back as written in the creating session and as an absolute path in later sessions; SourceField/SourceTable are "SRC" on its fields and the source's indexes (`IX_SRC_D`, `PK_SRC`) are listed; RecordCount is -1; `Updatable` is False in a read-only open and for a keyless link; `SELECT ... FROM MSysObjects` fails 3112; ADO `OpenSchema(adSchemaTables)` lists the link as LINK. Enumerating TableDefs (Name, Attributes, Connect, SourceTableName) does not open the source: it succeeds, with no error, while the source file holds text bytes or 16 zero bytes. The link's field list and index list are not stored in the host: they are read from the source when asked (a retained link shows the source's current columns, and with an unusable source the Fields collection has Count 0 and raises no error). DDL on a link (ADD COLUMN, CREATE INDEX) fails 3611, `DROP TABLE` of the link succeeds and leaves the source rows; an MDB host cannot link an ACCDB source (3845); an exclusively held source gives 3045 to a second opener and an absent one 3024.
- **Conditions**: ACCDB and MDB hosts, own sources made in the probe's work directory (the first-generation probes resolved the relative Connect to the creating attempt's folder; the repair and r-q-ado-misc probes use own sources).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-open-wrong-file/r-q-ado-misc.link-open-wrong-file.a97ef6c80a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-schema-change-own/r-q-ado-misc.link-schema-change-own.0d7fa7bc9b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/create-linked/r-enc-link.create-linked.92c44b92f7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/public-views/r-enc-link.public-views.a2e13199f0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/link-accdb-host-mdb-source/r-def-cat-txn.link-accdb-host-mdb-source.5085d85b58.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/link-mdb-host-accdb-source/r-def-cat-txn.link-mdb-host-accdb-source.e4fd09bc56.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/link-drop-delete-and-alter-link/r-def-cat-txn.link-drop-delete-and-alter-link.8d5c133d93.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/link-drop-delete-and-alter-refreshed/r-fix-cat-txn.link-drop-delete-and-alter-refreshed.d882644db3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/link-xproc-attach-exclusive-source/r-def-cat-txn.link-xproc-attach-exclusive-source.1280b0fb68.a1.json`. Discriminator for the path defect: `evidence/r-enc-link/meta-source-absent/r-enc-link.meta-source-absent.74deb41885.a1.json`.
- **Open**: the `IN` clause forms (all 3251; unresolved whether the source-type text is missing); an absent (deleted) source file (a file cannot be deleted by the probe layer).

### R-LINK-1 LINK-OPEN: what demands the source, and the errors
- **Claim**: With the source replaced by a non-database file (22 bytes of text, then 16 zero bytes) at the same path, the first calls that demand it are `OpenRecordset` on the link and `RefreshLink`: both fail 0x800A0D0F "Unrecognized database format '<source path>'" (Errors 3343); TableDefs enumeration and reading the link's Fields do not fail (Fields has Count 0). Earlier: `RefreshLink` with a missing file gives 3024, with the linked file itself 3011 (no table SRC), with Connect `garbage` 3170 "Could not find installable ISAM", with an unknown option (`;FOO=bar;DATABASE=...`) succeeds and Connect is stored verbatim; a wrong or missing PWD on a password-protected source gives 3031, and the `dbAttachSavePWD` bit clears after a successful `RefreshLink` with PWD. LK recordsets still open after each failed relink (the old connection is kept).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-open-wrong-file/r-q-ado-misc.link-open-wrong-file.a97ef6c80a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/link-relink-bad-targets-own-link/r-fix-cat-txn.link-relink-bad-targets-own-link.362caaf42a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/link-credentials/r-enc-link.link-credentials.08abb7d03d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/relink-bad-targets/r-enc-link.relink-bad-targets.ea64a21dcb.a1.json`.
- **Open (T)**: denied access, expired credential and ODBC/ISAM families (need ACLs, drivers or DSNs); a truly absent source.

### R-LINK-1 LINK-RELINK: RefreshLink and the index list
- **Claim**: With a link to source I (indexes `PK_SRC`, `IX_D`, `IX_V`), setting Connect to source N (primary key only) and calling `RefreshLink` leaves one index (`PK_SRC`) in the retained Indexes collection, a fresh one and a refreshed one alike; setting Connect back to source I and `RefreshLink` restores the three indexes. The link's index list follows the source; extra indexes on the old source are not kept. `RefreshLink` with an unchanged Connect changes nothing (earlier record); a retained dynaset opened before a relink keeps reading old rows and a new recordset reads the new source (earlier record, columns then typed from the new source).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-relink-index-preservation/r-q-ado-misc.link-relink-index-preservation.bb86b0af99.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/relink-refresh-unchanged/r-enc-link.relink-refresh-unchanged.9b0b5fb2a3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/relink-to-second-version/r-enc-link.relink-to-second-version.011cdf54df.a1.json`. Discriminator: the earlier probe's two sources both defined `IX_D`, so preservation could not be told from rebuild; here the second source lacks the extra indexes.
- **Open (T)**: indexes defined on the link itself (CREATE INDEX on a link fails 3611, the Access UI route is needed); native catalog byte deltas.

### R-LINK-1 LINK-SCHEMA: source schema changes seen through a link
- **Claim**: When the source is altered (column added and a column dropped) while no link object is open, reopening the host shows the new column list at once, before `RefreshLink`: the link's Fields (before and after `RefreshLink`) and a snapshot on the link both read ID, V, X with the new rows; `RefreshLink` adds nothing visible. While a link object holds the source, a schema change of the source from another Database fails 3211 ("could not lock table") and changes nothing (earlier record).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-schema-change-own/r-q-ado-misc.link-schema-change-own.0d7fa7bc9b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/link-schema-change-own-source/r-fix-cat-txn.link-schema-change-own-source.03e61c2c84.a1.json`.
- **Open (T)**: changes while a command is prepared or a cursor is held across processes; a type change and a replaced table.

### R-LINK-1 LINK-IDENTITY: duplicate rows in a keyless source
- **Claim**: A keyless link (no indexes) is `Updatable` False as a table, but a dynaset on it is Updatable True; with source rows (1,'x'), (2,'y'), (2,'y'), (2,'z') an Edit/Update of the second (2,'y') changes exactly that row (source then (2,'y'), (2,'Y2')), `Delete` of the last row removes (2,'z') only, and `UPDATE ... WHERE A = 2` over the remaining two rows reports RecordsAffected 2 and changes both. The row identity is the source row, not its values.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-identity-duplicates/r-q-ado-misc.link-identity-duplicates.d0912dfdb7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/link-keyless-duplicate-rows/r-fix-cat-txn.link-keyless-duplicate-rows.84ce48f09d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/link-keyless-source-updatable/r-def-cat-txn.link-keyless-source-updatable.8283670b75.a1.json`.
- **Open (T)**: a bookmark kept across a new session (needs a bookmark passed back); collated or nullable keys.

### R-LINK-1 LINK-PUSHDOWN: native lane link versus local
- **Claim**: In 8 query shapes (equality with `'a'` against an `'A'` row, IS NULL, LIKE, ORDER BY with Null and empty text, TOP 2 with ties, `CLng(N)`, DISTINCT, GROUP BY) a link to an ACCDB source and a local copy returned identical rows, order and errors: `TOP 2 ... ORDER BY D` returned the 3 tied rows in both; `CLng(N)` raised at `Field.Value` (row 2 0x800A0D88, Null row 0x800A005E), not at open.
- **Conditions**: native Access-file link only; no ODBC/ISAM family; demanded-call counting not possible.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-enc-link/pushdown-link-vs-local/r-enc-link.pushdown-link-vs-local.6ee2e5e064.a1.json`.
- **Open**: host-function demand counting and source request tracing (needs VBA or a trace: T); this record read the shared creation-dir source, but both lanes read the same unchanged data.


### R-LINK-1 LINK-UPDATE: updates through a native link
- **Claim**: Through a link UPDATE, INSERT (with and without the counter key; generated ID), DELETE and a 5000-character memo UPDATE report RecordsAffected 3/1/1/1/1 with Errors 0, visible in the link and in an independent read of the source (ID 3 deleted, 7 added, 1 edited); `AddNew/Update` gives `LastModified` and a new row; keyless links accept INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE, a predicate UPDATE over two rows gives RecordsAffected 2, and a recordset Edit of one duplicate row changes only that row (LINK-IDENTITY).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/link-update-own-source/r-fix-cat-txn.link-update-own-source.c558a4e672.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/link-keyless-duplicate-rows/r-fix-cat-txn.link-keyless-duplicate-rows.84ce48f09d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/link-keyless-source-updatable/r-def-cat-txn.link-keyless-source-updatable.8283670b75.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/link-accdb-host-mdb-source/r-def-cat-txn.link-accdb-host-mdb-source.5085d85b58.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-identity-duplicates/r-q-ado-misc.link-identity-duplicates.d0912dfdb7.a1.json`.
- **Open**: joined updates through two link occurrences; failing middle row with a link (T for fault injection).

### R-LINK-1 LINK-TXN: a native link takes part in the Workspace transaction, as seen from another Workspace and another process
- **Claim**: With a host holding a local table L and a link LK to an Access-file source: inside `BeginTrans` an INSERT into LK and into L raises both counts (LK 4 to 5, L 1 to 2) and a same-engine Database on the source reads 5; `Rollback` returns all three to the starting counts; a second transaction with `CommitTrans` keeps them (5, 2, 5); a nested pair with the inner `CommitTrans` and an outer `Rollback` undoes the inner commit (6 back to 5). The link behaves like a local table. A second Workspace does not see the first's uncommitted link insert and its own insert is refused 3188 (see LINK-SESSION). **Another process** opening the source file directly (shared, read-only, reopened for each read) reads 4 while the link's transaction is open (no lock error), 4 after the Rollback, and 5 at 24 ms after the second transaction's CommitTrans and 5 after 8 s: uncommitted link rows are invisible to another process and committed ones are visible at once.
- **Conditions**: Access-file source only; the same-engine reader sees its own Workspace's uncommitted work, the cross-process reader does not.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/link-txn-xproc/r-q2-ado-misc.link-txn-xproc.a17812db3d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-txn-own-source/r-q-ado-misc.link-txn-own-source.aba9442ddd.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-session-two-workspaces-own/r-q-ado-misc.link-session-two-workspaces-own.f62dc25f67.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/txn-local-and-link/r-enc-link.txn-local-and-link.f195b6751c.a1.json`.
- **Still T (one line)**: nontransactional or ODBC participants and failure or disconnect injection around each participant's accept.

### R-LINK-1 LINK-SESSION: two Workspaces on one link
- **Claim**: With the default Workspace and a second Workspace (`CreateWorkspace(..., dbUseJet)`) each holding the host: while the first has an open transaction with an inserted row, the second reads the old count (4 against 5), its own insert through the link fails 0x800A0C74 (3188) "Could not update; currently locked by another session on this machine"; after `Rollback` both read 4, after `CommitTrans` both read 5. Four runs (rollback and commit, with `IsolateODBCTrans` 0 and then 1; the property reads 0, then -1 after setting 1) gave the same results: the option makes no difference for an Access-file link.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/link-session-two-workspaces-own/r-q-ado-misc.link-session-two-workspaces-own.f62dc25f67.a1.json`, `evidence/r-enc-link/session-two-workspaces/r-enc-link.session-two-workspaces.2e3b61a0b1.a1.json`.
- **Open (T)**: source connection counts and credential identities; ODBC sources, where `IsolateODBCTrans` is meant to matter.

### R-COMPLEX-1 CX-DESC (public descriptor part)
- **Claim**: Through DAO a multivalue Text field appears as Field2 Type 109, Size 4, Attributes 1, `IsComplex` True, with a child recordset of one field `Value` (Type 10, SourceTable `T.Tags`); an attachment is Type 101 with child fields FileData 11, FileFlags 4, FileName 10, FileTimeStamp 8, FileType 10, FileURL 12; the eight kinds `CreateField` codes 102 to 109 (Byte, Integer, Long, Single, Double, GUID, Decimal, Text) create when TableDefs is refreshed (child fields Type 2 for Byte). Renaming the parent field and table, appending a multivalue Long and `ALTER TABLE DROP COLUMN` on a populated complex field are accepted and the group stays readable under the new names; `DROP COLUMN` removes the field from a refreshed Fields collection and compaction drops the complex column; `ADD COLUMN B LONG MULTIVALUE` creates a plain Long; ATTACHMENT, MULTIVALUE and COMPLEX DDL keywords fail 3292/3290.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/read-cx-mv-text/r-cplx-rebuild.read-cx-mv-text.833023e918.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/read-cx-attach/r-cplx-rebuild.read-cx-attach.5350cdaed9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/desc-rename-drop/r-cplx-rebuild.desc-rename-drop.3644cd1ae0.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/create-cx-types-accdb/r-fix-cat-txn.create-cx-types-accdb.d4240bae17.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/cx-ddl-complex-keywords/r-def-cat-txn.cx-ddl-complex-keywords.5a768c4a5b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/cx-drop-complex-column-refreshed/r-fix-cat-txn.cx-drop-complex-column-refreshed.fbd01cceae.a1.json`. Discriminator for the stale-collection defect: `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/cx-drop-complex-column-then-compact/r-def-cat-txn.cx-drop-complex-column-then-compact.4becf71f99.a1.json` versus the refreshed repair.
- **Open**: association rows (`MSysComplexColumns`) are unreadable through DAO (3112): raw-file work (T).

### R-COMPLEX-1 CX-READ: child surface, SQL and ADO views
- **Claim**: `Field2.Value` on a complex field returns a child Recordset (Type 2, RecordCount 1 before `MoveLast`, 2 after); `ParentRecordset` returns the parent; groups come out in insertion order (alpha, beta); empty groups give RecordCount 0 with BOF and EOF; a deleted parent gives 0x800A0BCD. SQL `SELECT Tags` returns an object (VT_DISPATCH, Type 109), `Tags.Value` joins one row per child, `Tags.Value = 'alpha'` matches the parents holding it, `GROUP BY Tags.Value` groups by child, `Att.FileName` counts the files while FileFlags/FileTimeStamp/FileURL are Null. Through ADO the column is Type 203 text "alpha;beta" (not a child object), `Tags.Value` rows are Type 202, and the ADO join order for two children is the reverse of DAO's.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/read-cx-mv-text/r-cplx-rebuild.read-cx-mv-text.833023e918.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/read-cx-sql-surface/r-cplx-rebuild.read-cx-sql-surface.ef2074a655.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/read-cx-ado/r-cplx-rebuild.read-cx-ado.f6ba571307.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/read-cx-types/r-cplx-rebuild.read-cx-types.2adde0682f.a1.json`.

### R-COMPLEX-1 CX-HOLD: held child objects and bookmark reuse
- **Claim**: After a parent `MoveNext`, `Requery`, `Delete`, or an INSERT, a held child recordset and its clone fail on RecordCount, BOF, EOF, Bookmark and Close with 0x800A0D5C and stay invalid when the parent moves back; `ParentRecordset` on it fails 0x800A0C93; reading `Field.Value` again yields a fresh child (p1 "alpha", p2 1 row, p3 empty). Closing the parent and Database, then the held child fails 0x800A0D5C; releasing Database and parent without Close leaves the held child readable; closing child, parent, Database in order succeeds. **Bookmarks** (new fixture, children alpha then beta of p1): a child Bookmark is a 4-byte array (`00030000` for the first child, `01030000` for the second); after the parent moved on, reading the old child's Bookmark gives 0x800A0D5C; a fresh child of p1 accepts the earlier bytes `00030000` and reads "alpha"; a made-up `09090909` fails 3001 (0x800A0BB9 "Invalid argument"); after the parent's `Requery` a fresh child of p1 refuses `00030000` with 3159 (0x800A0C57 "Not a valid bookmark") and its Value then fails 0x800A0BCD. The same bytes were accepted by p2's child and read "alpha", but p2's only child also holds "alpha", so whose row was selected is undecided.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/cx-hold-bookmark-reuse/r-q2-ado-misc.cx-hold-bookmark-reuse.74ff41217d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/create-cx/r-q2-ado-misc.create-cx.8ecb63d16d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/hold-surfaces-parent-movement/r-cplx-rebuild.hold-surfaces-parent-movement.4b59abed10.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/hold-parent-requery-delete-close/r-cplx-rebuild.hold-parent-requery-delete-close.a3f21effc7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/hold-release-orders/r-cplx-rebuild.hold-release-orders.c42662c73d.a1.json`.
- **Open**: whether a child bookmark identifies the row inside its group or across groups (a probe with distinct child values).

### R-COMPLEX-1 CX-EDIT: child edits, the parent edit state and Workspace transactions
- **Claim**: A child `AddNew` or `Edit` without a parent `Edit` gives 0x800A0F0C (later Value/Update/CancelUpdate 0x800A0BCC; Errors 3020), a child `Delete` without it succeeds; under parent `Edit` child AddNew/Edit/Update/Delete succeed; 300-character, Null, wrong-type or missing values end in 0x800A0BF2 at Update; a parent `Update` or `CancelUpdate` invalidates the held child (0x800A0D5C) and the child's update persists with `Update` but not with `CancelUpdate`; a duplicate multivalue value is refused 0x800A0EEC; the parent AutoNumber counter is consumed by a cancelled AddNew (IDs 100, 102 saved, 101 lost). Transactions: a child-only edit rolls back. **Commit variants** (parent recordset opened inside BeginTrans): parent `Edit`, child AddNew/Update, `CommitTrans` while the parent is still in Edit, then parent `Update`: no error, the child persists; child AddNew/Update, parent `Update`, then `CommitTrans`: no error, the child persists; both visible after a fresh read-only open of the file; a child AddNew with no parent Edit inside a transaction fails 0x800A0F0C and the commit stores nothing. The new child appears first in the group read (newest first: c1, beta, alpha). **Correction**: the draft's "a parent Update ends the level (3034 on Rollback)" came from a probe whose parent recordset was opened before BeginTrans; with it opened inside, the level survives the parent Update and CommitTrans works. The cause of the difference was not isolated.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/cx-edit-commit-variant/r-q2-ado-misc.cx-edit-commit-variant.bd22b3275b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/edit-child-admission/r-cplx-rebuild.edit-child-admission.5159d38c96.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/edit-parent-update-vs-cancel/r-cplx-rebuild.edit-parent-update-vs-cancel.9c0ac21604.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/edit-child-edit-delete/r-cplx-rebuild.edit-child-edit-delete.5fc3ce20e3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/edit-parent-addnew-counter/r-cplx-rebuild.edit-parent-addnew-counter.e79a0158aa.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/edit-workspace-transactions-child-only/r-fix-cat-txn.edit-workspace-transactions-child-only.8d946d51ec.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/edit-workspace-transactions-with-parent-update/r-fix-cat-txn.edit-workspace-transactions-with-parent-update.a4f082bee7.a1.json`.
- **Open**: the rollback variant with the parent recordset opened inside the transaction (to isolate the 3034 difference); generated child keys under rollback.

### R-COMPLEX-1 CX-ATT: attachment wrapper and file operations (DAO route)
- **Claim**: `FileData` through DAO is a wrapper `[len=0x14][flag][type length][type UTF-16]` followed by the content (20 bytes for "txt" plus content; 14 bytes with no extension); the second DWORD is 1 for every file loaded through `LoadFromFile`, including a compressible 20,000-byte file (FieldSize 20020, SaveToFile writes the 20,000 content bytes): the DAO route stores raw, not compressed. `FileFlags` is Null; direct assignment of `FileData` bytes fails 0x800A0BB9; `SaveToFile` onto an existing file fails 3839 and leaves it unchanged; a missing source gives 3024; the database file as source or destination fails 0x800C000E / 3839; an empty group gives 3021; case-differing names of one extension are refused (`ext.JPG` against `ext.jpg`).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/read-cx-attach/r-cplx-rebuild.read-cx-attach.5350cdaed9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/att-load-variants/r-cplx-rebuild.att-load-variants.c481913d20.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/att-replace-remove/r-cplx-rebuild.att-replace-remove.834dfbf4fb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/att-file-failures/r-cplx-rebuild.att-file-failures.3a21b847eb.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/att-file-failures-reacquired/r-fix-cat-txn.att-file-failures-reacquired.e2ec85ed63.a1.json`.
- **Open**: compressed forms (none arises from the DAO route); invalid header variations (byte work, T).

### R-COMPLEX-1 CX-CALC (part): calculated-field admission and recomputation
- **Claim**: On a table, expressions with Now, Date, Rnd, DLookup, DCount, Eval, Switch, an unknown function, `CLng([S])` and Format are refused 0x800A0F13 (3859); `[T].[A]` 3867 and `[X]+1` 3866; Len, `[A]/0`, `[A]*2147483647`, Mid and IIf are accepted. The stored result is computed at read: a held Field sees an UPDATE immediately (no Requery), overflow and division by zero surface at `Field.Value` (6 and 11), Null inputs give the expression's Null result. Setting `Field.Expression` on an existing calculated field fails 0x800A0C77; renaming a dependency is accepted and `SELECT *` then fails 0x800A0F2B and `ALTER COLUMN` of a dependent 0x800A0ED7; dropping a dependency succeeds. As queries the same expressions run (Now, Date, Rnd, DLookup) or fail 3085/3103/3464.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/calc-admission-refreshed/r-fix-cat-txn.calc-admission-refreshed.687e4f6a91.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/calc-source-data-change/r-cplx-rebuild.calc-source-data-change.cb67900037.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/calc-schema-change/r-cplx-rebuild.calc-schema-change.c1a2d8eff3.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/calc-admission/r-cplx-rebuild.calc-admission.5dd6393f7b.a1.json`.
- **Open**: stored bytes of the result and expression (byte-level, T).


### R-COMPLEX-1 CX-HISTORY (part): the AppendOnly property
- **Claim**: A Memo field made by DDL on an ACCDB has a Properties entry `AppendOnly` (Type 1, Value False; 24 properties); it is set to True without error, three UPDATEs of the memo then succeed (the memo reads the last value), and after reopening the database the property still reads True (24 properties); it is set back to False and reads False. The stored history is not readable through DAO or SQL (`ColumnHistory(...)` is not a function, 0x800A0C0D, earlier), so what the history keeps is not observed.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/cx-history-appendonly-persist/r-q-ado-misc.cx-history-appendonly-persist.22627f8f35.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/create-cx-history-accdb/r-fix-cat-txn.create-cx-history-accdb.f1375ec3f6.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/history-toggle-delete/r-cplx-rebuild.history-toggle-delete.a6a7435cca.a1.json`.
- **Open (T)**: history content, retention and deletion of the parent, compaction; the Access version-history UI.

### R-REBUILD-1 RB-CLEAN: compaction of valid files
- **Claim**: `CompactDatabase` of a valid same-format file gives an output of the same size (ACCDB 315392 B, MDB 221184 B in the format fixtures) with identical rows, relations and QueryDefs and only `LastUpdated` of two tables changing (one wide-row fixture grew 196608 to 294912 B); bloated files shrink (ACCDB 4444160 to 368640 B, MDB 4349952 to 274432 B), stably over three compactions with content unchanged; the AutoNumber counter restarts at max+1 (65 to 51); complex-field fixtures compact with identical group, row and export results; a link's Connect becomes an absolute path; hidden attributes and renames survive.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/compact-rich/r-cplx-rebuild.compact-rich.63e62e9d5d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/compact-bloat-repeated/r-cplx-rebuild.compact-bloat-repeated.240bb1d573.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/compact-counters/r-cplx-rebuild.compact-counters.6f7a3c7727.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/compact-complex-fixtures/r-cplx-rebuild.compact-complex-fixtures.1062bb62f5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/rb-compact-linked-database/r-def-cat-txn.rb-compact-linked-database.dd637da92d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/rb-compact-hidden-attributes/r-def-cat-txn.rb-compact-hidden-attributes.2b2fad159c.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fmt-2-pres-1/pres-compact/r-fmt-2-pres-1.pres-compact.7fb61c490f.a1.json`.
- **Open**: page/relocation tracking (byte-level, T); only `r-fmt-2-pres-1/pres-compact` shows the public catalog view.

### R-REBUILD-1 RB-OPTIONS: format, collation and password conversions
- **Claim**: ACCDB compacted with 128, 256 and 64 gives Version 12.0, 14.0 and 4.0 (to Jet4 `MSysComplexColumns` is dropped; fixtures with attachments or multivalue fields fail 0x800A0EEA); MDB to 64 is identical, to 128 gives 12.0 and adds `MSysCompactError` (MDB to ACCDB records "user-level security is no longer supported" for `MSysACEs`); MDB to 256 fails 0x800A0F4A; CollatingOrder 1033, 1034, 1053 from the locale string with Swedish ordering ä, ö after z and Spanish matching General for the original strings (the discriminating pair `ch`/`ll` shows Spanish differently); password and encrypt routes as in ENC-REKEY.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/options-version/r-cplx-rebuild.options-version.f066c63823.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/options-collation/r-cplx-rebuild.options-collation.bb64aba9b9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/options-collation-discriminating/r-fix-cat-txn.options-collation-discriminating.a2e512a738.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-cat-txn/compact-errors-table-singular/r-fix-cat-txn.compact-errors-table-singular.c396a6c77d.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/compact-complex-fixtures/r-cplx-rebuild.compact-complex-fixtures.1062bb62f5.a1.json`.

### R-REBUILD-1 RB-PUBLISH (part): destination rules and held sources
- **Claim**: An existing destination, a destination equal to the source (also upper-case, slash and dot spellings) and an existing alias give 3204 "Database already exists" with source and existing files unchanged; the `\\?\` spelling fails 3043. A source held by a shared Database fails 3704 (ACCDB, in and across processes); held exclusively, exclusive read-only, or by a native handle fails 3045 (MDB all four states 3045); no output file is produced in any case; a destination held by a Database fails 3204.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/publish-existing-and-aliases/r-cplx-rebuild.publish-existing-and-aliases.5fefb40786.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/publish-held-inproc/r-cplx-rebuild.publish-held-inproc.bf0f64c248.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/publish-held-xproc/r-cplx-rebuild.publish-held-xproc.d2bcaa30b9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-def-cat-txn/rb-compact-source-equals-destination/r-def-cat-txn.rb-compact-source-equals-destination.32f7eed1ee.a1.json`.
- **Open**: volumes and host replacement (paths are limited to input/output files: T).


### R-REBUILD-1 RB-REPAIR (part): RepairDatabase is refused whatever the file
- **Claim**: `DBEngine.RepairDatabase` fails 0x800A0CB3 / 3251 "Operation is not supported for this type of object" on a clean ACCDB copy, on a 22-byte text file and on an absent path, and leaves the files unchanged (clean fixture 200704 B and 8ce44b486c34 before and after; the text file 22 B); an `OpenDatabase` on the text file fails 3343 "Unrecognized database format", so the open-failure class and the repair refusal are separate. Earlier: the clean MDB gives 3251 too. ACE DAO therefore offers no repair route on this build; a damaged-file salvage can only be a different native route.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/rebuild-repair-variants/r-q-ado-misc.rebuild-repair-variants.1cdf0daaa8.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/repair-clean-control/r-cplx-rebuild.repair-clean-control.091ec86507.a1.json`, `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/negative-open-and-compact/r-cplx-rebuild.negative-open-and-compact.2a84c6675a.a1.json`.
- **Open (T)**: single-defect damaged copies (need byte writes) and the Access Compact & Repair command, which may repair where DAO refuses.

### R-REBUILD-1 RB-NEGATIVE: refusals that are not repair
- **Claim**: A missing source gives 3024; a text file or an empty file 3343 for both open and compact; a missing or wrong password 3031; an invalid locale 3228 with no output; an undefined option bit (1024) gives no error and an output. None of these refusals triggers a repair (RepairDatabase is refused separately, see RB-REPAIR). `DBEngine.Errors` is not cleared on success (stale 3343, 3228, 3204, 3420).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-cplx-rebuild/negative-open-and-compact/r-cplx-rebuild.negative-open-and-compact.2a84c6675a.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/rebuild-repair-variants/r-q-ado-misc.rebuild-repair-variants.1cdf0daaa8.a1.json`.
- **Open**: secured-workgroup refusal is NotModelled; transient I/O injection (T).

### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-ACT (part): provider identity, services and raw activation routes
- **Claim**: The ACE provider module is `ACEOLEDB.DLL` with `mso.dll`, `ACECORE.DLL`, `ACEWSTR.DLL`; after Open it reports Provider Name `MSJETOLEDB40.DLL` 04.00.8015, "Microsoft OLE DB Provider for Jet", DBMS "MS Jet" 04.00.0000, OLE DB 02.10; those named properties do not exist before Open (0x800A0CC1); Active Sessions stays 128 whatever the open connections; OLE DB Services -1, -2, -4, -5 and 0 all open and give client and server recordsets RecordCount 5; `ADOX.Catalog` creates and `JRO.JetEngine` fails 0x80040154. Raw activation of the data source class (ProgID `Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0`, CLSID {3BE786A0-0366-4F5C-9434-25CF162E475E}): CoCreateInstance with CLSCTX_INPROC_SERVER succeeds for IUnknown, IDBInitialize and IDBProperties (the object does not answer IDispatch, E_NOINTERFACE); CLSCTX_LOCAL_SERVER gives 0x80040154; with an outer IUnknown (aggregation) IUnknown succeeds and IDBInitialize fails 0x80040E22 (DB_E_NOAGGREGATION); `CoGetClassObject` for IClassFactory succeeds with LockServer, and `CreateInstance` fails E_NOINTERFACE (IDispatch asked) plain and 0x80040E22 aggregated. ADO's own Connection object answers IUnknown, IDispatch, ISupportErrorInfo and IConnectionPointContainer and refuses IDBInitialize, IDBProperties, IDBCreateSession, IDBInfo, IDBDataSourceAdmin and IPersist (E_NOINTERFACE), before and after Open.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ole-act-datasource-routes/r-q2-ado-misc.ole-act-datasource-routes.5e42b5bbdd.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q2-ado-misc/ole-act-datasource-interfaces/r-q2-ado-misc.ole-act-datasource-interfaces.d76129418f.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/act-provider-identity/r-oledb-odbc.act-provider-identity.25acbba9f2.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/act-services-variants/r-oledb-odbc.act-services-variants.2aaae78a8b.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/act-describe-companion-libraries/r-oledb-odbc.act-describe-companion-libraries.e074bad337.a1.json`, `evidence/r-str-ado-act/act-ado-classes/r-str-ado-act.act-ado-classes.a1382491b6.a2.json`, `evidence/r-ops/activation-routes/r-ops.activation-routes.ca3a2f32dc.a1.json`.
- **Still T (one line)**: the raw data source's interface inventory (the object cannot be bound without IDispatch, defect a), what an outer is asked for (none recorded) and initialization/final owners.

### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-PROP (ADO lane): property inventory and rowset requests
- **Claim**: 31 connection properties before Open and 101 after; Engine Type 6 for ACCDB, 5 for MDB; Isolation Levels 4096, Isolation Retention 9, Transaction DDL 16, Multiple Results 0; Mode 1 opens and UPDATE fails 0x80004005 "must use an updateable query", Modes 3, 16, 19 write, Modes 2, 5, 11, 15 fail Open 0x80040E21; `Jet OLEDB:Transaction Commit Mode=1` as a string key fails "Could not find installable ISAM"; Engine Type=5 on an ACCDB reads back 6; Command properties Maximum Rows, Immobile Rows, Literal Bookmarks refuse writes 0x80040E21, Bookmarkable=True gives a static cursor, Others' Changes Visible=True a keyset; requested-to-effective rowset tuples as in ADO-CURSOR.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/prop-connection-inventory/r-oledb-odbc.prop-connection-inventory.56c9930ca5.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/prop-modes/r-oledb-odbc.prop-modes.6dc4bf3de7.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/prop-jet-options/r-oledb-odbc.prop-jet-options.9b2779ebed.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/prop-rowset-requests/r-oledb-odbc.prop-rowset-requests.2862dd72da.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/prop-command-set-execute/r-oledb-odbc.prop-command-set-execute.bb46830709.a1.json`.
- **Open**: raw per-property status arrays and QueryInterface agreement need a native consumer (T).

### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-BUFFER (ADO lane, part): typed values and metadata
- **Claim**: ADO types are Long 3, Text/Code 202, Date 7, Memo 203 (size 536870910, `ActualSize` 24000 for 12000 characters), Binary 205 / 204 (16, zero-padded on read), Yes/No 11, Short 2, Byte 17, Single 4, GUID 72 (braced string), Decimal 131 (precision 38, scale 0); a server cursor reads Byte as VT_I2 and a client cursor as VT_UI1; empty binary reads Null and empty text ""; `OpenSchema(adSchemaColumns)` publishes ORDINAL_POSITION and COLUMN_FLAGS as declared type 19 but delivers VT_R8; MDB and ACCDB schema rows are equal.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/buffer-typed-server/r-oledb-odbc.buffer-typed-server.2ba9d301d4.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/buffer-typed-client/r-oledb-odbc.buffer-typed-client.66b48668ec.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/schema-columns/r-oledb-odbc.schema-columns.5088d7212a.a1.json`.

### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-FETCH (ADO lane): batch sizes, moves and limits
- **Claim**: Forward-only: CacheSize 1 and -1 accepted, 0/2/3/40 refused 0x800A0BB9, GetRows(0) 0x800A0C93, MovePrevious 0x800A0C93, Move(-1) and MoveLast 0x80040E24, MoveFirst after three MoveNext returns ID 1. Keyset: CacheSize 2, 3, 40, -1 accepted, 0 refused; GetRows(1), (2), (100) end at positions 2, 4 and EOF with position -3, RecordCount 32; Move(5) lands on ID 7, Move(100) on EOF, MovePrevious from EOF on ID 32, from the first row on BOF (position -2) and one more gives 0x800A0BCD.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/fetch-batches/r-oledb-odbc.fetch-batches.171708dbdc.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/fetch-moves/r-oledb-odbc.fetch-moves.5d65008d44.a1.json`, `evidence/r-fix-wire-ado/ado-bulk-elements/r-fix-wire-ado.ado-bulk-elements.04b87a6a04.a1.json`.
- **Open**: HROW arrays and `AddRefRows`/`ReleaseRows` (T).

### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-ROWLIFE (ADO lane): deleted rows, clones, Requery
- **Claim**: After `Delete` the row's Status is 262144 and reading a field gives 0x80040E23; `Resync` on that keyset gives 0x800A0CB3; after `Requery` a retained Field gives 0x800A0D5C; a clone keeps reading its row after the original recordset closes; `Record.Open(Recordset)` gives 0x800A0CB3 and `Record.Open` on a connection 0x800A0E7D.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/rowlife-clone-delete-requery/r-oledb-odbc.rowlife-clone-delete-requery.2e2d332056.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/stream-record-from-row/r-oledb-odbc.stream-record-from-row.a529dfd825.a1.json`.
- **Open**: retained bookmarks and accessors (T).

### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-STREAM (ADO lane): chunked long values
- **Claim**: `GetChunk(3)` on a 10-byte binary returns 3/3/3/1 then Null (server forward-only and client static), after which `Field.Value` fails 0x8000FFFF on forward-only; GetChunk 0 or -1 fails 0x800A0BB9; `GetChunk(65536)` returns all 12000 bytes; `AppendChunk` 3+4 stores 7 bytes, `AppendChunk(2)` onto a 10-byte value replaces it (2 bytes), on Text(50) fails 0x800A0C93, an empty chunk stores Null, and AddNew+AppendChunk then MoveNext stores the row.

- **Open**: `DBTYPE_IUNKNOWN` callbacks (T).


### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-PARAM (ADO lane, part): parameter order and mapping
- **Claim**: The ACE provider binds parameters by position for `?` markers and for a saved `PARAMETERS` query driven through ADO (names are accepted but not used; a missing parameter fails 0x80040E10 "Too few parameters. Expected 2.", a surplus one is ignored); `NamedParameters` True does not change that; the earlier "appending pA before pB fails" is a type mismatch of the positionally bound parameters; Output, InputOutput and ReturnValue directions on `?` commands fail 0x80040E21.

- **Open (T)**: multiple parameter sets with explicit stride and guarded gaps (ADO has one set per Execute); raw DBPARAMS.

### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-UPDATE (ADO lane): update errors, conflicts and another connection's writes
- **Claim**: "abc" into a Long fails at assignment 0x80020005, 3e9 fails 0x8002000A, an over-long text 0x80040E21 (3163); a unique conflict fails at Update (Status 4096, 3022); deleting a referenced row 3200; a missing required value (Status 131072, 3314) and a failed CHECK (3316); a stale optimistic Update fails 0x80040E21 (3197, Status 16) and CancelUpdate restores the stored value; on a deleted row assignment fails 3197 and a later read 0x80040E23; under pessimistic locking a second connection's UPDATE of the edited row fails 0x80004005 "currently locked" (3218) until Update or CancelUpdate, its SELECT reading the old value; A's Errors stay 0. A second connection's UPDATE, INSERT and DELETE are visible to a server keyset and a dynamic recordset after about 8 s (not at once) and to server static and client static recordsets only after `Requery` following that time; inserts show only after Requery (ADO-CURSOR).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/update-errors/r-oledb-odbc.update-errors.5fcd9e85e9.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/update-optimistic-conflict/r-oledb-odbc.update-optimistic-conflict.e8a28136fd.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/update-pessimistic-lock/r-oledb-odbc.update-pessimistic-lock.8e33a68e55.a1.json`, `evidence/r-q-ado-misc/cursor-external-visibility/r-q-ado-misc.cursor-external-visibility.2f25fa0c3d.a1.json`. Discriminator: `update-others-visible` (A sees nothing even after Requery) read without a pause; the new records show the same changes after 8 s.
- **Open**: allocation failure after an effect is a CalcDb fault-injection arm.

### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-TXN (ADO lane, part): held rows and DDL
- **Claim**: BeginTrans returns 1; held keyset, forward-only and client static recordsets stay open and readable across commit and rollback (Preserve on Commit true for all; on Abort false for the two server ones, true for client static); with Attributes 393216 the transaction stays open after commit or rollback and Close fails 0x800A0CAE; CREATE TABLE, INSERT and ALTER TABLE roll back and commit with the transaction; a second process's UPDATE of rows touched by an uncommitted transaction fails "currently locked" (3218) and its reads show neither the update nor the new row.
- **Records**: `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/txn-held-rows/r-oledb-odbc.txn-held-rows.91a0129038.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/txn-ddl-rollback/r-oledb-odbc.txn-ddl-rollback.b6e0df0330.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/txn-two-process/r-oledb-odbc.txn-two-process.6a3d7f3f3d.a1.json`.
- **Open**: B still reads the old value after A commits (needs a fresh connection or the 8 s pause pattern).

### R-OLEDB-1 OLE-ERROR (ADO lane, part): error channels
- **Claim**: Each provider failure leaves exactly one Errors entry (never accumulating) with Number, Description, SQLState, NativeError and help context; ADO-raised errors leave Errors empty; a retained Error keeps its fields after Clear, Command release, Close and collection release; open failures: absent file 0x80004005 (3024), no Data Source 0x80040E4D (source "Provider"), Excel/Text extended properties 3274/3044, User ID/Password 0x80040E4D (3028), Mode 99 0x800A0C93 (Open then uses 16).
- **Records**: `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/error-record-channels/r-oledb-odbc.error-record-channels.529a1b1cbf.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/error-retained-lifetime/r-oledb-odbc.error-retained-lifetime.c57e77d481.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/error-open-failures/r-oledb-odbc.error-open-failures.f7cd849588.a1.json`, `evidence/r-oledb-odbc/error-ado-origin/r-oledb-odbc.error-ado-origin.54086c2722.a1.json`.
- **Open**: raw `IErrorRecords` order and locale lookup (T).
